ОБНАРУЖЕННЫЕ ПРОБЛЕМЫ И ОГРАНИЧЕНИЯ:
❌ Локальная модель 7B-14B на M4 24GB не справляется со сложными процессами:

Не может задавать уточняющие вопросы

Не может обрабатывать многошаговый диалог

Не может генерировать сложные конфигурации узлов

❌ Агенты часто не содержат обязательного поля task → выполнение падает

❌ Нет библиотеки переиспользуемых шаблонов процессов

❌ Нет атомарных скиллов для композиции сложных процессов

⚠️ Железо: M4 24GB → локальные LLM 7B-14B работают на пределе

@🧠 Специалист по знаниям + @🏛️ Хранитель архитектуры:

Создать систему шаблонов процессов:

Дизайн структуры шаблона:

yaml
template:
  id: telegram_collector
  name: "Telegram News Collector"
  description: "Собирает сообщения из TG каналов"
  inputs:
    - bot_token: string (required, secret)
    - channels: list (required)
    - keywords: list (optional)
  outputs:
    - messages: string
    - count: integer
  steps:
    - id: fetch
      type: http_node
      config:
        url: "https://api.telegram.org/bot${bot_token}/getUpdates"
    - id: parse
      type: agent
      config:
        task: "Извлечь тексты сообщений"
  schedule: optional
API для управления шаблонами:

GET /api/v1/templates/list — список шаблонов

POST /api/v1/templates/create — создание

GET /api/v1/templates/{id} — получение

DELETE /api/v1/templates/{id} — удаление

Интеграция с генерацией процессов:

При генерации модель ищет подходящий шаблон

Если найден — разворачивает в процесс

Если не найден — анализирует возможность композиции

## КОМАНДА 1/5 (ШАГ 1) — @🏛️ Хранитель архитектуры

**Добавьте в конец файла `ai-farm/agents/batuta/process_models.py` следующие классы:**

```python
# ========== ШАБЛОНЫ (ДОБАВЛЕНО ЭТАП 84) ==========

@dataclass
class TemplateInput:
    """Входной параметр шаблона"""
    name: str
    type: str  # string, integer, boolean, list, secret
    required: bool = True
    default: Any = None
    description: str = ""


@dataclass
class TemplateOutput:
    """Выходной параметр шаблона"""
    name: str
    type: str
    description: str = ""


@dataclass
class Template:
    """Шаблон процесса"""
    id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    version: str
    inputs: List[TemplateInput]
    outputs: List[TemplateOutput]
    steps: List[Dict[str, Any]]  # список узлов процесса
    schedule: Optional[str] = None
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    
    def to_yaml(self) -> str:
        """Экспорт шаблона в YAML"""
        data = {
            'template': {
                'id': self.id,
                'name': self.name,
                'description': self.description,
                'version': self.version,
                'inputs': [vars(inp) for inp in self.inputs],
                'outputs': [vars(out) for out in self.outputs],
                'steps': self.steps,
                'schedule': self.schedule,
                'tags': self.tags
            }
        }
        return yaml.dump(data, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False)
    
    @classmethod
    def from_yaml(cls, yaml_str: str) -> 'Template':
        """Импорт шаблона из YAML"""
        data = yaml.safe_load(yaml_str)
        template_data = data['template']
        
        inputs = [TemplateInput(**inp) for inp in template_data.get('inputs', [])]
        outputs = [TemplateOutput(**out) for out in template_data.get('outputs', [])]
        
        return cls(
            id=template_data['id'],
            name=template_data['name'],
            description=template_data['description'],
            version=template_data['version'],
            inputs=inputs,
            outputs=outputs,
            steps=template_data.get('steps', []),
            schedule=template_data.get('schedule'),
            tags=template_data.get('tags', [])
        )
```

**И добавьте новый метод в класс ProcessDB (перед `get_process` или в конец класса):**

```python
    # ========== МЕТОДЫ ДЛЯ ШАБЛОНОВ (ЭТАП 84) ==========
    
    def save_template(self, template_id: str, name: str, description: str, 
                      yaml_content: str, version: str = '1.0.0', 
                      tags: List[str] = None, inputs: List[Dict] = None,
                      outputs: List[Dict] = None, schedule: str = None) -> bool:
        """Save template to database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            tags_json = json.dumps(tags or [])
            inputs_json = json.dumps(inputs or [])
            outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs or [])
            
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO templates 
                (id, name, description, version, yaml, tags, inputs, outputs, schedule, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """, (template_id, name, description, version, yaml_content, 
                  tags_json, inputs_json, outputs_json, schedule))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving template: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_template(self, template_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """Get template from database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM templates WHERE id = ?", (template_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return None
            
            template = {
                'id': row['id'],
                'name': row['name'],
                'description': row['description'],
                'version': row['version'],
                'yaml': row['yaml'],
                'tags': json.loads(row['tags']) if row['tags'] else [],
                'inputs': json.loads(row['inputs']) if row['inputs'] else [],
                'outputs': json.loads(row['outputs']) if row['outputs'] else [],
                'schedule': row['schedule'],
                'created_at': row['created_at'],
                'updated_at': row['updated_at']
            }
            
            conn.close()
            return template
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting template: {e}")
            return None
    
    def list_templates(self, tag: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all templates, optionally filtered by tag"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            if tag:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, version, tags, schedule, created_at, updated_at
                    FROM templates 
                    WHERE tags LIKE ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """, (f'%"{tag}"%',))
            else:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, version, tags, schedule, created_at, updated_at
                    FROM templates 
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """)
            
            templates = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            for t in templates:
                if t.get('tags'):
                    t['tags'] = json.loads(t['tags'])
                else:
                    t['tags'] = []
            
            conn.close()
            return templates
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error listing templates: {e}")
            return []
    
    def delete_template(self, template_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a template"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM templates WHERE id = ?", (template_id,))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error deleting template: {e}")
            return False
```

"""
Database models for Process Designer with Project integration
"""
import json
import sqlite3
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

class ProcessDB:
    """Database manager for processes"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/processes.db"):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """Initialize database tables with project support"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # Create tables with project_id
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS processes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                yaml TEXT NOT NULL,
                mermaid TEXT,
                variables TEXT,
                project_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
                project_path TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        """)
        
        # Add columns if they don't exist (for existing databases)
        try:
            cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE processes ADD COLUMN project_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            pass  # column already exists
        
        try:
            cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE processes ADD COLUMN project_path TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            pass  # column already exists
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_nodes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                position_x INTEGER NOT NULL,
                position_y INTEGER NOT NULL,
                data TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                UNIQUE(process_id, node_id)
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_edges (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                source TEXT NOT NULL,
                target TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_executions (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                task_id TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                current_node TEXT,
                started_at TIMESTAMP,
                completed_at TIMESTAMP,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def save_process(self, process_id: str, name: str, description: str, 
                     nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], yaml: str, mermaid: str,
                     variables: Dict = None, project_id: str = None, project_path: str = None) -> bool:
        """Save process to database with project support"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            variables_json = json.dumps(variables or {})
            
            # Insert process with project fields
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO processes (id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables, project_id, project_path, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """, (process_id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables_json, project_id, project_path))
            
            # Delete old nodes and edges
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            
            # Insert nodes
            for node in nodes:
                node_id = f"{process_id}_{node['id']}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_nodes (id, process_id, node_id, node_type, label, position_x, position_y, data)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    node_id, process_id, node['id'], 
                    node.get('type', 'default'), 
                    node.get('data', {}).get('label', ''),
                    node.get('position', {}).get('x', 0), 
                    node.get('position', {}).get('y', 0),
                    json.dumps(node.get('data', {}))
                ))
            
            # Insert edges
            for edge in edges:
                edge_source = edge['source']
                edge_target = edge['target']
                edge_id_val = edge.get('id', f"{edge_source}-{edge_target}")
                edge_id = f"{process_id}_{edge_id_val}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_edges (id, process_id, source, target)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (edge_id, process_id, edge_source, edge_target))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving process: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_process(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """Get process from database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return None
            
            # Get nodes
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            nodes = []
            for node_row in cursor.fetchall():
                nodes.append({
                    'id': node_row['node_id'],
                    'type': node_row['node_type'],
                    'position': {'x': node_row['position_x'], 'y': node_row['position_y']},
                    'data': json.loads(node_row['data']) if node_row['data'] else {}
                })
            
            # Get edges
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            edges = []
            for edge_row in cursor.fetchall():
                edge_full_id = edge_row['id']
                edge_short_id = edge_full_id.replace(f"{process_id}_", "")
                edges.append({
                    'id': edge_short_id,
                    'source': edge_row['source'],
                    'target': edge_row['target']
                })
            
            conn.close()
            
            return {
                'id': row['id'],
                'name': row['name'],
                'description': row['description'],
                'yaml': row['yaml'],
                'mermaid': row['mermaid'],
                'variables': json.loads(row['variables']) if row['variables'] else {},
                'project_id': row['project_id'],
                'project_path': row['project_path'],
                'nodes': nodes,
                'edges': edges,
                'created_at': row['created_at'],
                'updated_at': row['updated_at']
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting process: {e}")
            return None
    
    def list_processes(self, project_id: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all processes, optionally filtered by project_id"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            if project_id:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, project_id, project_path, created_at, updated_at 
                    FROM processes 
                    WHERE project_id = ? 
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """, (project_id,))
            else:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, project_id, project_path, created_at, updated_at 
                    FROM processes 
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """)
            
            processes = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            conn.close()
            return processes
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error listing processes: {e}")
            return []
    
    def get_processes_by_project(self, project_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get all processes for a specific project (alias for list_processes with filter)"""
        return self.list_processes(project_id=project_id)
    
    def delete_process(self, process_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a process"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error deleting process: {e}")
            return False

# Singleton
_db_instance = None

def get_process_db():
    global _db_instance
    if _db_instance is None:
        _db_instance = ProcessDB()
    return _db_instance

# История выполнения процессов
class ExecutionHistory:
    def __init__(self):
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute('''
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS execution_history (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                process_name TEXT,
                status TEXT,
                start_time TEXT,
                end_time TEXT,
                duration REAL,
                nodes_count INTEGER,
                results TEXT,
                error TEXT,
                created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        ''')
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def save_execution(self, execution_id: str, process_id: str, process_name: str, 
                       status: str, start_time: str, nodes_count: int = 0):
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute('''
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO execution_history 
            (id, process_id, process_name, status, start_time, nodes_count)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        ''', (execution_id, process_id, process_name, status, start_time, nodes_count))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def update_execution(self, execution_id: str, status: str, end_time: str, 
                         duration: float, results: dict = None, error: str = None):
        import sqlite3
        import json
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        results_json = json.dumps(results) if results else None
        
        cursor.execute('''
            UPDATE execution_history 
            SET status = ?, end_time = ?, duration = ?, results = ?, error = ?
            WHERE id = ?
        ''', (status, end_time, duration, results_json, error, execution_id))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def get_history(self, process_id: str = None, limit: int = 50):
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        if process_id:
            cursor.execute('''
                SELECT * FROM execution_history 
                WHERE process_id = ? 
                ORDER BY created_at DESC 
                LIMIT ?
            ''', (process_id, limit))
        else:
            cursor.execute('''
                SELECT * FROM execution_history 
                ORDER BY created_at DESC 
                LIMIT ?
            ''', (limit,))
        
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        conn.close()
        
        history = []
        for row in rows:
            history.append({
                "id": row[0],
                "process_id": row[1],
                "process_name": row[2],
                "status": row[3],
                "start_time": row[4],
                "end_time": row[5],
                "duration": row[6],
                "nodes_count": row[7],
                "results": row[8],
                "error": row[9],
                "created_at": row[10]
            })
        return history

# Глобальный экземпляр
_execution_history = None

def get_execution_history():
    global _execution_history
    if _execution_history is None:
        _execution_history = ExecutionHistory()
    return _execution_history%

```python
class ProcessDB:

                variables TEXT,
                project_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL,

        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            pass  # column already exists

                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,

    def save_template(self, template_id: str, name: str, description: str, 
                      yaml_content: str, version: str = '1.0.0', 
                      tags: List[str] = None, inputs: List[Dict] = None,
                      outputs: List[Dict] = None, schedule: str = None) -> bool:

                (id, name, description, version, yaml, tags, inputs, outputs, schedule, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """, (template_id, name, description, version, yaml_content, 
                  tags_json, inputs_json, outputs_json, schedule))

            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM templates WHERE id = ?", (template_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return None
            
            template = {
                'id': row['id'],
                'name': row['name'],
                'description': row['description'],
                'version': row['version'],
                'yaml': row['yaml'],
                'tags': json.loads(row['tags']) if row['tags'] else [],
                'inputs': json.loads(row['inputs']) if row['inputs'] else [],
                'outputs': json.loads(row['outputs']) if row['outputs'] else [],
                'schedule': row['schedule'],
                'created_at': row['created_at'],

            }
            
            conn.close()
            return template
        except Exception as e:

    def list_templates(self, tag: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all templates, optionally filtered by tag"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            if tag:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, version, tags, schedule, created_at, updated_at
                    FROM templates 
                    WHERE tags LIKE ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """, (f'%"{tag}"%',))
            else:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, version, tags, schedule, created_at, updated_at
                    FROM templates 
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """)
            
            templates = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            for t in templates:
                if t.get('tags'):
                    t['tags'] = json.loads(t['tags'])
                else:
                    t['tags'] = []
            
            conn.close()
            return templates
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error listing templates: {e}")
            return []

            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM templates WHERE id = ?", (template_id,))
```

"""
Database models for Process Designer with Project integration
"""
import json
import sqlite3
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

class ProcessDB:
    """Database manager for processes"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/processes.db"):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """Initialize database tables with project support"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # Create tables with project_id
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS processes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                yaml TEXT NOT NULL,
                mermaid TEXT,
                variables TEXT,
                project_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
                project_path TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        """)
        
        # Add columns if they don't exist (for existing databases)
        try:
            cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE processes ADD COLUMN project_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            pass  # column already exists
        
        try:
            cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE processes ADD COLUMN project_path TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            pass  # column already exists
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_nodes (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                node_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                label TEXT NOT NULL,
                position_x INTEGER NOT NULL,
                position_y INTEGER NOT NULL,
                data TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                UNIQUE(process_id, node_id)
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_edges (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                source TEXT NOT NULL,
                target TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_executions (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                task_id TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                current_node TEXT,
                started_at TIMESTAMP,
                completed_at TIMESTAMP,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY(process_id) REFERENCES processes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
            )
        """)
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def save_process(self, process_id: str, name: str, description: str, 
                     nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], yaml: str, mermaid: str,
                     variables: Dict = None, project_id: str = None, project_path: str = None) -> bool:
        """Save process to database with project support"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            variables_json = json.dumps(variables or {})
            
            # Insert process with project fields
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO processes (id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables, project_id, project_path, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            """, (process_id, name, description, yaml, mermaid, variables_json, project_id, project_path))
            
            # Delete old nodes and edges
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            
            # Insert nodes
            for node in nodes:
                node_id = f"{process_id}_{node['id']}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_nodes (id, process_id, node_id, node_type, label, position_x, position_y, data)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    node_id, process_id, node['id'], 
                    node.get('type', 'default'), 
                    node.get('data', {}).get('label', ''),
                    node.get('position', {}).get('x', 0), 
                    node.get('position', {}).get('y', 0),
                    json.dumps(node.get('data', {}))
                ))
            
            # Insert edges
            for edge in edges:
                edge_source = edge['source']
                edge_target = edge['target']
                edge_id_val = edge.get('id', f"{edge_source}-{edge_target}")
                edge_id = f"{process_id}_{edge_id_val}"
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO process_edges (id, process_id, source, target)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (edge_id, process_id, edge_source, edge_target))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving process: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_process(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """Get process from database"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return None
            
            # Get nodes
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_nodes WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            nodes = []
            for node_row in cursor.fetchall():
                nodes.append({
                    'id': node_row['node_id'],
                    'type': node_row['node_type'],
                    'position': {'x': node_row['position_x'], 'y': node_row['position_y']},
                    'data': json.loads(node_row['data']) if node_row['data'] else {}
                })
            
            # Get edges
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM process_edges WHERE process_id = ?", (process_id,))
            edges = []
            for edge_row in cursor.fetchall():
                edge_full_id = edge_row['id']
                edge_short_id = edge_full_id.replace(f"{process_id}_", "")
                edges.append({
                    'id': edge_short_id,
                    'source': edge_row['source'],
                    'target': edge_row['target']
                })
            
            conn.close()
            
            return {
                'id': row['id'],
                'name': row['name'],
                'description': row['description'],
                'yaml': row['yaml'],
                'mermaid': row['mermaid'],
                'variables': json.loads(row['variables']) if row['variables'] else {},
                'project_id': row['project_id'],
                'project_path': row['project_path'],
                'nodes': nodes,
                'edges': edges,
                'created_at': row['created_at'],
                'updated_at': row['updated_at']
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting process: {e}")
            return None
    
    def list_processes(self, project_id: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all processes, optionally filtered by project_id"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            if project_id:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, project_id, project_path, created_at, updated_at 
                    FROM processes 
                    WHERE project_id = ? 
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """, (project_id,))
            else:
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, name, description, project_id, project_path, created_at, updated_at 
                    FROM processes 
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                """)
            
            processes = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            conn.close()
            return processes
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error listing processes: {e}")
            return []
    
    def get_processes_by_project(self, project_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get all processes for a specific project (alias for list_processes with filter)"""
        return self.list_processes(project_id=project_id)
    
    def delete_process(self, process_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a process"""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error deleting process: {e}")
            return False

# Singleton
_db_instance = None

def get_process_db():
    global _db_instance
    if _db_instance is None:
        _db_instance = ProcessDB()
    return _db_instance

# История выполнения процессов
class ExecutionHistory:
    def __init__(self):
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute('''
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS execution_history (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                process_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                process_name TEXT,
                status TEXT,
                start_time TEXT,
                end_time TEXT,
                duration REAL,
                nodes_count INTEGER,
                results TEXT,
                error TEXT,
                created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        ''')
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def save_execution(self, execution_id: str, process_id: str, process_name: str, 
                       status: str, start_time: str, nodes_count: int = 0):
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute('''
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO execution_history 
            (id, process_id, process_name, status, start_time, nodes_count)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        ''', (execution_id, process_id, process_name, status, start_time, nodes_count))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def update_execution(self, execution_id: str, status: str, end_time: str, 
                         duration: float, results: dict = None, error: str = None):
        import sqlite3
        import json
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        results_json = json.dumps(results) if results else None
        
        cursor.execute('''
            UPDATE execution_history 
            SET status = ?, end_time = ?, duration = ?, results = ?, error = ?
            WHERE id = ?
        ''', (status, end_time, duration, results_json, error, execution_id))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def get_history(self, process_id: str = None, limit: int = 50):
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect('processes.db')
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        if process_id:
            cursor.execute('''
                SELECT * FROM execution_history 
                WHERE process_id = ? 
                ORDER BY created_at DESC 
                LIMIT ?
            ''', (process_id, limit))
        else:
            cursor.execute('''
                SELECT * FROM execution_history 
                ORDER BY created_at DESC 
                LIMIT ?
            ''', (limit,))
        
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        conn.close()
        
        history = []
        for row in rows:
            history.append({
                "id": row[0],
                "process_id": row[1],
                "process_name": row[2],
                "status": row[3],
                "start_time": row[4],
                "end_time": row[5],
                "duration": row[6],
                "nodes_count": row[7],
                "results": row[8],
                "error": row[9],
                "created_at": row[10]
            })
        return history

# Глобальный экземпляр
_execution_history = None

def get_execution_history():
    global _execution_history
    if _execution_history is None:
        _execution_history = ExecutionHistory()
    return _execution_history

```python
"""
API for Template Management (Stage 84)
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

from .process_models import get_process_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/templates", tags=["Templates"])

# ========== Pydantic Models ==========

class TemplateInput(BaseModel):
    name: str
    type: str = "string"
    required: bool = True
    default: Any = None
    description: str = ""

class TemplateOutput(BaseModel):
    name: str
    type: str = "string"
    description: str = ""

class TemplateCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=200)
    description: str = ""
    version: str = "1.0.0"
    yaml: str = Field(..., description="YAML definition of the template")
    tags: List[str] = []
    inputs: List[TemplateInput] = []
    outputs: List[TemplateOutput] = []
    schedule: Optional[str] = None

class TemplateUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    description: Optional[str] = None
    version: Optional[str] = None
    yaml: Optional[str] = None
    tags: Optional[List[str]] = None
    inputs: Optional[List[TemplateInput]] = None
    outputs: Optional[List[TemplateOutput]] = None
    schedule: Optional[str] = None

class TemplateResponse(BaseModel):
    id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    version: str
    tags: List[str]
    inputs: List[dict]
    outputs: List[dict]
    schedule: Optional[str]
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str

class TemplateListResponse(BaseModel):
    total: int
    templates: List[dict]

# ========== API Endpoints ==========

@router.post("/create", response_model=TemplateResponse)
async def create_template(template: TemplateCreate):
    """Create a new template"""
    db = get_process_db()
    template_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    
    inputs_dict = [inp.dict() for inp in template.inputs]
    outputs_dict = [out.dict() for out in template.outputs]
    
    success = db.save_template(
        template_id=template_id,
        name=template.name,
        description=template.description,
        yaml_content=template.yaml,
        version=template.version,
        tags=template.tags,
        inputs=inputs_dict,
        outputs=outputs_dict,
        schedule=template.schedule
    )
    
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to create template")
    
    saved = db.get_template(template_id)
    if not saved:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Template created but not found")
    
    return TemplateResponse(
        id=saved['id'],
        name=saved['name'],
        description=saved['description'],
        version=saved['version'],
        tags=saved['tags'],
        inputs=saved['inputs'],
        outputs=saved['outputs'],
        schedule=saved['schedule'],
        created_at=saved['created_at'],
        updated_at=saved['updated_at']
    )

@router.get("/list", response_model=TemplateListResponse)
async def list_templates(
    tag: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Filter by tag"),
    limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0)
):
    """List all templates with optional filtering"""
    db = get_process_db()
    templates = db.list_templates(tag=tag)
    
    # Apply pagination
    total = len(templates)
    paginated = templates[offset:offset + limit]
    
    return TemplateListResponse(
        total=total,
        templates=paginated
    )

@router.get("/{template_id}", response_model=TemplateResponse)
async def get_template(template_id: str):
    """Get template by ID"""
    db = get_process_db()
    template = db.get_template(template_id)
    
    if not template:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Template {template_id} not found")
    
    return TemplateResponse(
        id=template['id'],
        name=template['name'],
        description=template['description'],
        version=template['version'],
        tags=template['tags'],
        inputs=template['inputs'],
        outputs=template['outputs'],
        schedule=template['schedule'],
        created_at=template['created_at'],
        updated_at=template['updated_at']
    )

@router.put("/{template_id}")
async def update_template(template_id: str, template: TemplateUpdate):
    """Update existing template"""
    db = get_process_db()
    existing = db.get_template(template_id)
    
    if not existing:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Template {template_id} not found")
    
    # Merge updates
    updated_name = template.name if template.name is not None else existing['name']
    updated_description = template.description if template.description is not None else existing['description']
    updated_version = template.version if template.version is not None else existing['version']
    updated_yaml = template.yaml if template.yaml is not None else existing['yaml']
    updated_tags = template.tags if template.tags is not None else existing['tags']
    updated_inputs = [inp.dict() for inp in template.inputs] if template.inputs is not None else existing['inputs']
    updated_outputs = [out.dict() for out in template.outputs] if template.outputs is not None else existing['outputs']
    updated_schedule = template.schedule if template.schedule is not None else existing['schedule']
    
    success = db.save_template(
        template_id=template_id,
        name=updated_name,
        description=updated_description,
        yaml_content=updated_yaml,
        version=updated_version,
        tags=updated_tags,
        inputs=updated_inputs,
        outputs=updated_outputs,
        schedule=updated_schedule
    )
    
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to update template")
    
    return {"status": "success", "message": f"Template {template_id} updated"}

@router.delete("/{template_id}")
async def delete_template(template_id: str):
    """Delete template by ID"""
    db = get_process_db()
    existing = db.get_template(template_id)
    
    if not existing:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Template {template_id} not found")
    
    success = db.delete_template(template_id)
    
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to delete template")
    
    return {"status": "success", "message": f"Template {template_id} deleted"}

@router.post("/{template_id}/instantiate")
async def instantiate_template(
    template_id: str,
    project_id: Optional[str] = None,
    variables: Dict[str, Any] = {}
):
    """Instantiate template into a process"""
    db = get_process_db()
    template = db.get_template(template_id)
    
    if not template:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Template {template_id} not found")
    
    # Parse YAML and substitute variables
    import yaml
    try:
        template_yaml = yaml.safe_load(template['yaml'])
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Invalid YAML: {str(e)}")
    
    # Generate process ID
    process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    
    # Apply variable substitution to steps
    process_yaml = _substitute_variables(template_yaml, variables)
    process_yaml_str = yaml.dump(process_yaml)
    
    # Save as process
    success = db.save_process(
        process_id=process_id,
        name=f"{template['name']} (from template)",
        description=template['description'],
        nodes=[],  # Will be populated by process designer
        edges=[],
        yaml=process_yaml_str,
        mermaid="",  # Will be generated
        project_id=project_id
    )
    
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to instantiate process")
    
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "process_id": process_id,
        "process_name": f"{template['name']} (from template)"
    }

def _substitute_variables(template_data: dict, variables: dict) -> dict:
    """Substitute ${variable_name} placeholders with values"""
    import re
    
    def replace_vars(obj):
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            return {k: replace_vars(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
        elif isinstance(obj, list):
            return [replace_vars(item) for item in obj]
        elif isinstance(obj, str):
            # Replace ${variable} patterns
            pattern = r'\$\{([^}]+)\}'
            matches = re.findall(pattern, obj)
            result = obj
            for match in matches:
                if match in variables:
                    result = result.replace(f'${{{match}}}', str(variables[match]))
            return result
        else:
            return obj
    
    return replace_vars(template_data)
```

## КОМАНДА 3/5 (ШАГ 3) — @🚀 Хранитель стабильности

**Создайте реестр атомарных скиллов в файле:**

`ai-farm/agents/batuta/skills_registry.py`

```python
"""
Atomic Skills Registry (Stage 84)
Библиотека переиспользуемых атомарных скиллов
"""
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

class SkillExecutor(str, Enum):
    HTTP_NODE = "http_node"
    BASH = "bash"
    FILE_NODE = "file_node"
    AGENT = "agent"
    PYTHON = "python"

@dataclass
class SkillInput:
    name: str
    type: str  # string, integer, boolean, dict, list
    required: bool = True
    default: Any = None
    description: str = ""

@dataclass
class SkillOutput:
    name: str
    type: str
    description: str = ""

@dataclass
class AtomicSkill:
    """Атомарный скилл - минимальная переиспользуемая единица"""
    name: str
    description: str
    executor: SkillExecutor
    inputs: List[SkillInput]
    outputs: List[SkillOutput]
    config_template: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def to_node_config(self, input_values: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Преобразует скилл в конфигурацию узла процесса"""
        # Заменяем переменные в шаблоне
        config = self.config_template.copy()
        
        for input_def in self.inputs:
            if input_def.name in input_values:
                # Рекурсивно подставляем значение
                self._set_nested_value(config, input_def.name, input_values[input_def.name])
            elif input_def.required:
                raise ValueError(f"Required input '{input_def.name}' not provided")
        
        return config
    
    def _set_nested_value(self, config: dict, path: str, value: Any):
        """Устанавливает значение по вложенному пути (например, 'url.timeout')"""
        parts = path.split('.')
        current = config
        for part in parts[:-1]:
            if part not in current:
                current[part] = {}
            current = current[part]
        current[parts[-1]] = value


# ========== РЕЕСТР СКИЛЛОВ ==========

class SkillsRegistry:
    """Реестр всех доступных атомарных скиллов"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._skills: Dict[str, AtomicSkill] = {}
        self._init_default_skills()
    
    def _init_default_skills(self):
        """Инициализация стандартных скиллов"""
        
        # 1. HTTP GET
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="http_get",
            description="Выполняет HTTP GET запрос",
            executor=SkillExecutor.HTTP_NODE,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("url", "string", required=True, description="URL запроса"),
                SkillInput("headers", "dict", required=False, default={}, description="HTTP заголовки"),
                SkillInput("timeout", "integer", required=False, default=30, description="Таймаут в секундах")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("body", "string", description="Тело ответа"),
                SkillOutput("status", "integer", description="HTTP статус код"),
                SkillOutput("headers", "dict", description="Заголовки ответа")
            ],
            config_template={
                "method": "GET",
                "url": "${url}",
                "headers": "${headers}",
                "timeout": "${timeout}"
            }
        ))
        
        # 2. HTTP POST
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="http_post",
            description="Выполняет HTTP POST запрос",
            executor=SkillExecutor.HTTP_NODE,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("url", "string", required=True, description="URL запроса"),
                SkillInput("body", "string", required=False, default="", description="Тело запроса"),
                SkillInput("headers", "dict", required=False, default={}, description="HTTP заголовки"),
                SkillInput("timeout", "integer", required=False, default=30, description="Таймаут в секундах")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("body", "string", description="Тело ответа"),
                SkillOutput("status", "integer", description="HTTP статус код"),
                SkillOutput("headers", "dict", description="Заголовки ответа")
            ],
            config_template={
                "method": "POST",
                "url": "${url}",
                "body": "${body}",
                "headers": "${headers}",
                "timeout": "${timeout}"
            }
        ))
        
        # 3. Bash Execute
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="bash_exec",
            description="Выполняет bash команду",
            executor=SkillExecutor.BASH,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("command", "string", required=True, description="Команда для выполнения"),
                SkillInput("cwd", "string", required=False, default=".", description="Рабочая директория"),
                SkillInput("env", "dict", required=False, default={}, description="Переменные окружения")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("stdout", "string", description="Вывод команды"),
                SkillOutput("stderr", "string", description="Ошибки команды"),
                SkillOutput("exit_code", "integer", description="Код возврата")
            ],
            config_template={
                "command": "${command}",
                "cwd": "${cwd}",
                "env": "${env}"
            }
        ))
        
        # 4. File Read
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="file_read",
            description="Читает файл из файловой системы",
            executor=SkillExecutor.FILE_NODE,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("path", "string", required=True, description="Путь к файлу"),
                SkillInput("encoding", "string", required=False, default="utf-8", description="Кодировка файла")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("content", "string", description="Содержимое файла"),
                SkillOutput("size", "integer", description="Размер файла в байтах")
            ],
            config_template={
                "operation": "read",
                "path": "${path}",
                "encoding": "${encoding}"
            }
        ))
        
        # 5. File Write
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="file_write",
            description="Записывает данные в файл",
            executor=SkillExecutor.FILE_NODE,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("path", "string", required=True, description="Путь к файлу"),
                SkillInput("content", "string", required=True, description="Содержимое для записи"),
                SkillInput("append", "boolean", required=False, default=False, description="Добавить в конец файла")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("success", "boolean", description="Успех операции"),
                SkillOutput("path", "string", description="Путь к файлу")
            ],
            config_template={
                "operation": "write",
                "path": "${path}",
                "content": "${content}",
                "append": "${append}"
            }
        ))
        
        # 6. JSON Parse
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="json_parse",
            description="Парсит JSON строку и извлекает значение по пути",
            executor=SkillExecutor.AGENT,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("json_string", "string", required=True, description="JSON строка"),
                SkillInput("path", "string", required=False, default="", description="Путь в JSON (например, data.user.name)")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("value", "any", description="Извлеченное значение"),
                SkillOutput("parsed", "dict", description="Полный распарсенный JSON")
            ],
            config_template={
                "task": "Parse JSON and extract value at path '${path}'. JSON: ${json_string}",
                "output_key": "value"
            }
        ))
        
        # 7. Text Search (grep)
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="text_search",
            description="Ищет паттерн в тексте",
            executor=SkillExecutor.BASH,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("text", "string", required=True, description="Текст для поиска"),
                SkillInput("pattern", "string", required=True, description="Регулярное выражение"),
                SkillInput("case_sensitive", "boolean", required=False, default=True, description="Учитывать регистр")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("matches", "list", description="Найденные совпадения"),
                SkillOutput("count", "integer", description="Количество совпадений")
            ],
            config_template={
                "command": "echo '${text}' | grep '${pattern}'",
                "cwd": "."
            }
        ))
        
        # 8. Python Execute
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="python_exec",
            description="Выполняет Python код",
            executor=SkillExecutor.PYTHON,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("code", "string", required=True, description="Python код"),
                SkillInput("input_data", "any", required=False, default=None, description="Входные данные")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("result", "any", description="Результат выполнения")
            ],
            config_template={
                "code": "${code}",
                "input": "${input_data}"
            }
        ))
        
        # 9. Sleep/Delay
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="sleep",
            description="Ожидание заданное количество секунд",
            executor=SkillExecutor.BASH,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("seconds", "integer", required=True, default=1, description="Количество секунд")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("waited", "integer", description="Сколько секунд ожидали")
            ],
            config_template={
                "command": "sleep ${seconds} && echo 'Waited ${seconds} seconds'"
            }
        ))
        
        # 10. Date/Time
        self.register(AtomicSkill(
            name="datetime",
            description="Получает текущую дату и время",
            executor=SkillExecutor.BASH,
            inputs=[
                SkillInput("format", "string", required=False, default="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", description="Формат даты")
            ],
            outputs=[
                SkillOutput("datetime", "string", description="Текущая дата и время")
            ],
            config_template={
                "command": "date '${format}'"
            }
        ))
    
    def register(self, skill: AtomicSkill):
        """Регистрирует новый скилл"""
        self._skills[skill.name] = skill
    
    def get(self, name: str) -> Optional[AtomicSkill]:
        """Получает скилл по имени"""
        return self._skills.get(name)
    
    def list_all(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Возвращает список всех скиллов для API"""
        return [
            {
                "name": skill.name,
                "description": skill.description,
                "executor": skill.executor.value,
                "inputs": [{"name": inp.name, "type": inp.type, "required": inp.required, "description": inp.description} 
                          for inp in skill.inputs],
                "outputs": [{"name": out.name, "type": out.type, "description": out.description} 
                           for out in skill.outputs],
                "tags": skill.tags
            }
            for skill in self._skills.values()
        ]
    
    def list_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Возвращает скиллы по тегу"""
        return [
            skill_info for skill_info in self.list_all()
            if tag in skill_info.get("tags", [])
        ]
    
    def search(self, query: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Поиск скиллов по названию или описанию"""
        query_lower = query.lower()
        results = []
        for skill in self._skills.values():
            if query_lower in skill.name.lower() or query_lower in skill.description.lower():
                results.append({
                    "name": skill.name,
                    "description": skill.description,
                    "executor": skill.executor.value
                })
        return results


# Глобальный экземпляр реестра
_skills_registry: Optional[SkillsRegistry] = None

def get_skills_registry() -> SkillsRegistry:
    """Получить глобальный реестр скиллов"""
    global _skills_registry
    if _skills_registry is None:
        _skills_registry = SkillsRegistry()
    return _skills_registry
```

## КОМАНДА 4/5 (ШАГ 4) — @🧠 Специалист по знаниям

**Создайте механизм композиции и обработки неизвестных требований в файле:**

`ai-farm/agents/batuta/template_resolver.py`

```python
"""
Template Resolver with Composition & Unknown Requirements Handling (Stage 84)
"""
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
import yaml
import json
from .skills_registry import get_skills_registry, AtomicSkill
from .process_models import get_process_db

class ResolutionStatus(str, Enum):
    FOUND = "found"
    CANDIDATE = "candidate"
    NEEDS_HELP = "needs_help"
    IMPOSSIBLE = "impossible"

@dataclass
class Requirement:
    """Требование к процессу"""
    description: str
    input_types: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    output_types: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
@dataclass
class ResolutionResult:
    """Результат поиска/композиции"""
    status: ResolutionStatus
    template_id: Optional[str] = None
    template_name: Optional[str] = None
    composed_skills: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    missing_capabilities: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    suggested_template: Optional[Dict] = None
    message: str = ""


class TemplateResolver:
    """Поиск и композиция шаблонов"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.skills_registry = get_skills_registry()
        self.db = get_process_db()
    
    def resolve(self, requirement: str, context: Dict = None) -> ResolutionResult:
        """
        Основной метод: найти или собрать шаблон под требование
        """
        context = context or {}
        
        # 1. Поиск готового шаблона
        template = self._find_matching_template(requirement)
        if template:
            return ResolutionResult(
                status=ResolutionStatus.FOUND,
                template_id=template['id'],
                template_name=template['name'],
                message=f"Найден готовый шаблон: {template['name']}"
            )
        
        # 2. Пытаемся собрать из скиллов
        composition = self._compose_from_skills(requirement)
        if composition['success']:
            # Создаем шаблон-кандидат
            candidate = self._create_candidate_template(requirement, composition['skills'])
            return ResolutionResult(
                status=ResolutionStatus.CANDIDATE,
                composed_skills=composition['skills'],
                suggested_template=candidate,
                message=f"Собран из {len(composition['skills'])} скиллов. Сохранить как шаблон?"
            )
        
        # 3. Не хватает возможностей - запрашиваем помощь
        missing = composition.get('missing', [])
        suggested = self._suggest_template_structure(requirement, missing)
        
        return ResolutionResult(
            status=ResolutionStatus.NEEDS_HELP,
            missing_capabilities=missing,
            suggested_template=suggested,
            message=f"Не хватает возможностей: {', '.join(missing)}. Требуется помощь пользователя."
        )
    
    def _find_matching_template(self, requirement: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """Поиск подходящего шаблона по описанию"""
        templates = self.db.list_templates()
        
        requirement_lower = requirement.lower()
        
        # Простой поиск по ключевым словам
        best_match = None
        best_score = 0
        
        for template in templates:
            score = 0
            name_score = sum(1 for word in requirement_lower.split() 
                           if word in template['name'].lower())
            desc_score = sum(1 for word in requirement_lower.split() 
                           if word in template['description'].lower())
            
            score = name_score * 3 + desc_score
            
            if score > best_score and score > 0:
                best_score = score
                best_match = template
        
        return best_match if best_score >= 2 else None
    
    def _compose_from_skills(self, requirement: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Композиция процесса из атомарных скиллов"""
        all_skills = self.skills_registry.list_all()
        requirement_lower = requirement.lower()
        
        # Находим релевантные скиллы
        relevant_skills = []
        for skill in all_skills:
            if (skill['name'] in requirement_lower or 
                any(word in requirement_lower for word in skill['description'].lower().split()[:5])):
                relevant_skills.append(skill['name'])
        
        if relevant_skills:
            return {
                'success': True,
                'skills': relevant_skills[:5],  # максимум 5 скиллов
                'missing': []
            }
        
        # Определяем недостающие возможности
        missing = self._detect_missing_capabilities(requirement)
        
        return {
            'success': False,
            'skills': [],
            'missing': missing
        }
    
    def _detect_missing_capabilities(self, requirement: str) -> List[str]:
        """Определяет недостающие возможности по требованию"""
        requirement_lower = requirement.lower()
        missing = []
        
        # База знаний о возможных недостающих интеграциях
        capability_keywords = {
            "oracle": ["oracle", "database", "sql", "db"],
            "telegram": ["telegram", "tg", "bot", "messenger"],
            "slack": ["slack", "channel", "webhook"],
            "google_sheets": ["google", "sheets", "spreadsheet", "gdrive"],
            "aws": ["aws", "s3", "lambda", "ec2"],
            "docker": ["docker", "container", "k8s", "kubernetes"],
            "redis": ["redis", "cache", "pubsub"],
            "mongodb": ["mongo", "mongodb", "nosql"]
        }
        
        for capability, keywords in capability_keywords.items():
            if any(kw in requirement_lower for kw in keywords):
                missing.append(capability)
        
        return missing
    
    def _suggest_template_structure(self, requirement: str, missing: List[str]) -> Dict:
        """Предлагает структуру шаблона на основе требования"""
        # Определяем тип операции
        operation_type = "unknown"
        if any(word in requirement.lower() for word in ["collect", "fetch", "get", "read"]):
            operation_type = "collector"
        elif any(word in requirement.lower() for word in ["send", "post", "write", "save"]):
            operation_type = "sender"
        elif any(word in requirement.lower() for word in ["sync", "copy", "migrate"]):
            operation_type = "synchronizer"
        elif any(word in requirement.lower() for word in ["parse", "extract", "transform"]):
            operation_type = "transformer"
        
        # Генерируем имя
        name = f"{operation_type}_{'_'.join(missing[:2])}" if missing else f"{operation_type}_custom"
        
        return {
            "name": name,
            "description": requirement[:200],
            "inputs": ["input_data", "config"],
            "outputs": ["output_data", "status"],
            "missing_capabilities": missing,
            "suggested_steps": [
                {"step": 1, "action": f"Fetch data from {missing[0] if missing else 'source'}"},
                {"step": 2, "action": "Process data"},
                {"step": 3, "action": f"Send to {missing[1] if len(missing) > 1 else 'destination'}"}
            ] if missing else []
        }
    
    def _create_candidate_template(self, requirement: str, skills: List[str]) -> Dict:
        """Создает шаблон-кандидат из скиллов"""
        import uuid
        
        # Строим YAML из скиллов
        steps = []
        for i, skill_name in enumerate(skills):
            skill = self.skills_registry.get(skill_name)
            if skill:
                steps.append({
                    "id": f"step_{i+1}",
                    "name": skill_name,
                    "type": skill.executor.value,
                    "config": skill.config_template,
                    "next": f"step_{i+2}" if i < len(skills) - 1 else None
                })
        
        template_yaml = {
            "template": {
                "id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "name": f"auto_candidate_{requirement[:30].replace(' ', '_')}",
                "description": requirement,
                "version": "0.1.0",
                "status": "candidate",
                "steps": steps,
                "tags": ["auto-generated", "candidate"]
            }
        }
        
        return {
            "yaml": yaml.dump(template_yaml),
            "skills_used": skills,
            "status": "candidate"
        }
    
    def save_candidate_template(self, candidate: Dict, user_approved: bool = False) -> Optional[str]:
        """Сохраняет шаблон-кандидат (если одобрен пользователем)"""
        if not user_approved:
            return None
        
        try:
            template_data = yaml.safe_load(candidate['yaml'])
            template_info = template_data['template']
            
            template_id = template_info['id']
            success = self.db.save_template(
                template_id=template_id,
                name=template_info['name'],
                description=template_info.get('description', ''),
                yaml_content=candidate['yaml'],
                version=template_info.get('version', '0.1.0'),
                tags=template_info.get('tags', ['candidate']),
                inputs=[],
                outputs=[]
            )
            
            return template_id if success else None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving candidate template: {e}")
            return None


class HelpRequestHandler:
    """Обработчик запросов помощи пользователю"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def format_help_request(result: ResolutionResult) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Форматирует запрос помощи для UI"""
        return {
            "alert": "Не найден шаблон для задачи",
            "level": "warning",
            "message": result.message,
            "missing_capabilities": result.missing_capabilities,
            "suggested_template": result.suggested_template,
            "actions": [
                {"id": "create", "label": "Создать новый шаблон", "primary": True},
                {"id": "skip", "label": "Пропустить", "primary": False},
                {"id": "use_cloud_llm", "label": "Использовать облачную LLM", "primary": False},
                {"id": "cancel", "label": "Отмена", "primary": False}
            ]
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def process_user_action(action: str, result: ResolutionResult, user_input: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Обрабатывает действие пользователя"""
        if action == "create":
            return {
                "action": "open_template_editor",
                "template": result.suggested_template,
                "message": "Открыт редактор шаблонов"
            }
        elif action == "use_cloud_llm":
            return {
                "action": "switch_to_cloud_llm",
                "message": "Переключение на облачную LLM для сложной генерации"
            }
        elif action == "skip":
            return {
                "action": "continue_manual",
                "message": "Продолжить ручное создание процесса"
            }
        else:
            return {
                "action": "cancelled",
                "message": "Операция отменена"
            }


# Глобальный экземпляр
_resolver: Optional[TemplateResolver] = None

def get_template_resolver() -> TemplateResolver:
    """Получить глобальный резолвер"""
    global _resolver
    if _resolver is None:
        _resolver = TemplateResolver()
    return _resolver
```
