`tactical-command-v3/src/features/templates/`

### 5.1 — Создайте файл `TemplateList.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Box,
  Button,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Typography,
  Grid,
  Chip,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
  TextField,
  MenuItem,
  CircularProgress,
  Alert
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Add as AddIcon,
  Delete as DeleteIcon,
  Edit as EditIcon,
  PlayArrow as PlayIcon,
  Search as SearchIcon
} from '@mui/icons-material';

interface Template {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  version: string;
  tags: string[];
  schedule?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8000/api/v1/templates';

export const TemplateList: React.FC = () => {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<Template[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedTemplate, setSelectedTemplate] = useState<Template | null>(null);
  const [searchTag, setSearchTag] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTemplates();
  }, [searchTag]);

  const loadTemplates = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const url = searchTag ? `${API_BASE}/list?tag=${searchTag}` : `${API_BASE}/list`;
      const response = await fetch(url);
      const data = await response.json();
      setTemplates(data.templates || []);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Ошибка загрузки шаблонов');
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!selectedTemplate) return;
    
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/${selectedTemplate.id}`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      
      if (response.ok) {
        await loadTemplates();
        setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
        setSelectedTemplate(null);
      } else {
        setError('Ошибка удаления шаблона');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Ошибка удаления шаблона');
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  const handleInstantiate = async (template: Template) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/${template.id}/instantiate`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({})
      });
      
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        alert(`Процесс создан: ${data.process_id}`);
        // TODO: Navigate to process designer
      } else {
        setError('Ошибка создания процесса из шаблона');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Ошибка создания процесса');
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <Box display="flex" justifyContent="center" p={4}>
        <CircularProgress />
      </Box>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Box p={3}>
      {/* Header */}
      <Box display="flex" justifyContent="space-between" alignItems="center" mb={3}>
        <Typography variant="h4">Библиотека шаблонов</Typography>
        <Button
          variant="contained"
          startIcon={<AddIcon />}
          onClick={() => {/* TODO: Open template editor */}}
        >
          Создать шаблон
        </Button>
      </Box>

      {/* Search */}
      <Box display="flex" gap={2} mb={3}>
        <TextField
          label="Поиск по тегу"
          value={searchTag}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchTag(e.target.value)}
          size="small"
          sx={{ width: 300 }}
        />
        <Button
          variant="outlined"
          startIcon={<SearchIcon />}
          onClick={() => loadTemplates()}
        >
          Поиск
        </Button>
      </Box>

      {/* Error Alert */}
      {error && (
        <Alert severity="error" onClose={() => setError(null)} sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
          {error}
        </Alert>
      )}

      {/* Templates Grid */}
      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {templates.map((template) => (
          <Grid item xs={12} md={6} lg={4} key={template.id}>
            <Card variant="outlined">
              <CardContent>
                <Box display="flex" justifyContent="space-between" alignItems="flex-start">
                  <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                    {template.name}
                  </Typography>
                  <Box>
                    <IconButton size="small" onClick={() => handleInstantiate(template)}>
                      <PlayIcon fontSize="small" />
                    </IconButton>
                    <IconButton size="small">
                      <EditIcon fontSize="small" />
                    </IconButton>
                    <IconButton 
                      size="small" 
                      color="error"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setSelectedTemplate(template);
                        setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
                      }}
                    >
                      <DeleteIcon fontSize="small" />
                    </IconButton>
                  </Box>
                </Box>
                
                <Typography variant="body2" color="textSecondary" gutterBottom>
                  {template.description || 'Нет описания'}
                </Typography>
                
                <Box display="flex" gap={1} mt={2} flexWrap="wrap">
                  <Chip label={`v${template.version}`} size="small" variant="outlined" />
                  {template.tags?.map((tag) => (
                    <Chip key={tag} label={tag} size="small" color="primary" variant="outlined" />
                  ))}
                  {template.schedule && (
                    <Chip label={`⏰ ${template.schedule}`} size="small" color="secondary" />
                  )}
                </Box>
                
                <Typography variant="caption" color="textSecondary" display="block" sx={{ mt: 2 }}>
                  Обновлен: {new Date(template.updated_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                </Typography>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </Grid>
        ))}
      </Grid>

      {/* Empty State */}
      {templates.length === 0 && !loading && (
        <Box textAlign="center" py={8}>
          <Typography variant="h6" color="textSecondary">
            Нет шаблонов
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="textSecondary">
            Создайте первый шаблон или сохраните существующий процесс как шаблон
          </Typography>
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* Delete Dialog */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>Удалить шаблон?</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            Вы уверены, что хотите удалить шаблон "{selectedTemplate?.name}"?
            Это действие необратимо.
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>Отмена</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDelete} color="error" variant="contained">
            Удалить
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Box>
  );
};
```

### 5.2 — Создайте файл `TemplateEditor.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Box,
  Button,
  TextField,
  Typography,
  Paper,
  IconButton,
  Alert,
  Chip,
  Stack
} from '@mui/material';
import { Add as AddIcon, Close as CloseIcon } from '@mui/icons-material';

interface TemplateFormData {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  version: string;
  yaml: string;
  tags: string[];
  schedule: string;
}

const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8000/api/v1/templates';

interface TemplateEditorProps {
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: () => void;
  initialData?: any;
}

export const TemplateEditor: React.FC<TemplateEditorProps> = ({ onClose, onSaved, initialData }) => {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<TemplateFormData>({
    name: initialData?.name || '',
    description: initialData?.description || '',
    version: initialData?.version || '1.0.0',
    yaml: initialData?.yaml || '# YAML определение шаблона\ntemplate:\n  id: my_template\n  name: "Мой шаблон"\n  steps: []',
    tags: initialData?.tags || [],
    schedule: initialData?.schedule || ''
  });
  const [newTag, setNewTag] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const handleAddTag = () => {
    if (newTag && !formData.tags.includes(newTag)) {
      setFormData({ ...formData, tags: [...formData.tags, newTag] });
      setNewTag('');
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveTag = (tag: string) => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, tags: formData.tags.filter(t => t !== tag) });
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!formData.name.trim()) {
      setError('Название шаблона обязательно');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/create`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: formData.name,
          description: formData.description,
          version: formData.version,
          yaml: formData.yaml,
          tags: formData.tags,
          schedule: formData.schedule || null
        })
      });

      if (response.ok) {
        onSaved();
      } else {
        const data = await response.json();
        setError(data.detail || 'Ошибка сохранения шаблона');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Ошибка соединения с сервером');
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Paper sx={{ p: 3, maxWidth: 800, mx: 'auto', my: 2 }}>
      <Box display="flex" justifyContent="space-between" alignItems="center" mb={2}>
        <Typography variant="h5">
          {initialData ? 'Редактировать шаблон' : 'Создать шаблон'}
        </Typography>
        <IconButton onClick={onClose}>
          <CloseIcon />
        </IconButton>
      </Box>

      {error && (
        <Alert severity="error" onClose={() => setError(null)} sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
          {error}
        </Alert>
      )}

      <Stack spacing={2}>
        <TextField
          label="Название"
          value={formData.name}
          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
          required
          fullWidth
        />

        <TextField
          label="Описание"
          value={formData.description}
          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, description: e.target.value })}
          multiline
          rows={2}
          fullWidth
        />

        <TextField
          label="Версия"
          value={formData.version}
          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, version: e.target.value })}
          fullWidth
          size="small"
        />

        <TextField
          label="Расписание (cron)"
          value={formData.schedule}
          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, schedule: e.target.value })}
          placeholder="0 9 * * *"
          fullWidth
          size="small"
          helperText="Формат cron: minute hour day month weekday"
        />

        <Box>
          <Typography variant="subtitle2">Теги</Typography>
          <Box display="flex" gap={1} mb={1} flexWrap="wrap">
            {formData.tags.map((tag) => (
              <Chip
                key={tag}
                label={tag}
                onDelete={() => handleRemoveTag(tag)}
                size="small"
              />
            ))}
          </Box>
          <Box display="flex" gap={1}>
            <TextField
              size="small"
              placeholder="Новый тег"
              value={newTag}
              onChange={(e) => setNewTag(e.target.value)}
              onKeyPress={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleAddTag()}
            />
            <Button variant="outlined" onClick={handleAddTag} startIcon={<AddIcon />}>
              Добавить
            </Button>
          </Box>
        </Box>

        <TextField
          label="YAML определение"
          value={formData.yaml}
          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, yaml: e.target.value })}
          multiline
          rows={12}
          fullWidth
          fontFamily="monospace"
        />

        <Box display="flex" gap={2} justifyContent="flex-end">
          <Button onClick={onClose}>Отмена</Button>
          <Button
            variant="contained"
            onClick={handleSave}
            disabled={saving || !formData.name.trim()}
          >
            {saving ? 'Сохранение...' : 'Сохранить'}
          </Button>
        </Box>
      </Stack>
    </Paper>
  );
};
```

```python
"""
API for Atomic Skills (Stage 84)
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, Query
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from .skills_registry import get_skills_registry

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/skills", tags=["Skills"])

@router.get("/list")
async def list_skills(
    tag: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Filter by tag"),
    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search by name/description")
):
    """List all available atomic skills"""
    registry = get_skills_registry()
    
    skills = registry.list_all()
    
    # Filter by tag
    if tag:
        skills = [s for s in skills if tag in s.get("tags", [])]
    
    # Filter by search
    if search:
        search_lower = search.lower()
        skills = [s for s in skills if search_lower in s["name"].lower() or search_lower in s["description"].lower()]
    
    return {
        "total": len(skills),
        "skills": skills
    }

@router.get("/{skill_name}")
async def get_skill(skill_name: str):
    """Get skill details by name"""
    registry = get_skills_registry()
    skill = registry.get(skill_name)
    
    if not skill:
        return {"error": f"Skill '{skill_name}' not found"}, 404
    
    return {
        "name": skill.name,
        "description": skill.description,
        "executor": skill.executor.value,
        "inputs": [{"name": inp.name, "type": inp.type, "required": inp.required, "description": inp.description} for inp in skill.inputs],
        "outputs": [{"name": out.name, "type": out.type, "description": out.description} for out in skill.outputs],
        "config_template": skill.config_template
    }
```

allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

class AgentStatus(str, Enum):
    IDLE = "idle"
    BUSY = "busy"
    ERROR = "error"
    OFFLINE = "offline"

class Agent(BaseModel):
    id: Optional[str] = None
    name: str
    type: str
    status: AgentStatus
    endpoint: Optional[str] = None
    last_seen: datetime
    metrics: Dict[str, Any] = {}

class Service(BaseModel):
    name: str
    type: str
    endpoint: str
    port: int
    status: str
    last_check: datetime

class HITLTask(BaseModel):
    id: Optional[str] = None
    title: str
    description: str
    status: str = "pending"
    created_at: datetime
    assigned_to: Optional[str] = None
    data: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    result: Optional[Dict] = None
    approved_by: Optional[str] = None
    action: Optional[str] = None

# In-memory storage
agents = {}
services = {}
hitl_tasks = {}

@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_event():
    # Регистрируем API сервисы
    services["clipping_api"] = Service(
        name="clipping_api",
        type="api",
        endpoint="http://localhost:8011",
        port=8011,
        status="registered",
        last_check=datetime.now()
    )
    services["search_api"] = Service(
        name="search_api",
        type="api",
        endpoint="http://localhost:8003",
        port=8003,
        status="registered",
        last_check=datetime.now()
    )
    services["graph_api"] = Service(
        name="graph_api",
        type="api",
        endpoint="http://localhost:8004",
        port=8004,
        status="registered",
        last_check=datetime.now()
    )
        
    # Загружаем задачи из БД
    try:
        from task_history import load_all_tasks
        loaded_tasks = load_all_tasks()
        for task_data in loaded_tasks:
            task = HITLTask(**task_data)
            hitl_tasks[task.id] = task
        logger.info(f"Loaded {len(loaded_tasks)} tasks from database")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Could not load tasks from DB: {e}")
    
    # Архивируем задачи старше 7 дней
    archived_count = 0
    seven_days_ago = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7)
    for task in hitl_tasks.values():
        if task.created_at < seven_days_ago and task.status not in ['archived']:
            task.status = 'archived'
            archived_count += 1
    if archived_count > 0:
        logger.info(f"Archived {archived_count} old tasks")    

    # Создаем тестовых агентов
    agents["agent_1"] = Agent(
        id="clipper_1",
        name="Clipping Processor",
        type="clipping",
        status=AgentStatus.IDLE,
        endpoint="http://localhost:8011",
        last_seen=datetime.now(),
        metrics={"processed": 150}
    )
    agents["agent_2"] = Agent(
        id="searcher_1",
        name="Search Agent",
        type="search",
        status=AgentStatus.BUSY,
        endpoint="http://localhost:8003",
        last_seen=datetime.now(),
        metrics={"queries": 45}
    )

    agents["integrator_1"] = Agent(
        id="integrator_1",
        name="Integration Agent",
        type="integrator",
        status=AgentStatus.IDLE,
        endpoint=None,
        last_seen=datetime.now(),
        metrics={"merges": 0, "patches": 0}
    )
    agents["agent_3"] = Agent(
        id="analyzer_1",
        name="Graph Analyzer",
        type="graph",
        status=AgentStatus.IDLE,
        endpoint="http://localhost:8004",
        last_seen=datetime.now(),
        metrics={"nodes": 581}
    )
    logger.info(f"Batuta started with {len(services)} services and {len(agents)} agents")

@app.get("/")
async def root():
    return {
        "status": "Batuta ready",
        "version": "0.2.0",
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "endpoints": ["/health", "/api/v1/agents", "/api/v1/services", "/api/v1/hitl/tasks"]
    }

@app.get("/health")
async def health():
    return {
        "status": "healthy",
        "agents": len(agents),
        "services": len(services),
        "tasks_pending": len([t for t in hitl_tasks.values() if t.status == "pending"])
    }

@app.get("/api/v1/agents")
async def get_agents():
    return list(agents.values())

@app.get("/api/v1/services")
async def get_services():
    return list(services.values())

@app.post("/api/v1/hitl/tasks")
async def create_hitl_task(task: HITLTask):
    start_time = time.time()
    log_task(task.id, "created", 0, {"title": task.title, "description": task.description[:100] if task.description else ""})
    if not task.id or task.id == "string" or task.id.startswith("temp_"):
        task.id = f"task_{len(hitl_tasks) + 1}"
    task.created_at = datetime.now()
    hitl_tasks[task.id] = task
    # Синхронизация с Neo4j (TaskGraph)
    try:
        create_task_node_sync(task.id, task.title, task.description, task.status)
        logger.info(f"Task {task.id} synced to Neo4j")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to sync task {task.id} to Neo4j: {e}")
    logger.info(f"Created HITL task: {task.id}")
    duration = time.time() - start_time
    log_task(task.id, "created_complete", duration, {"title": task.title})    
    return task

@app.get("/api/v1/hitl/tasks")
async def get_hitl_tasks(status: Optional[str] = None):
    tasks = [t for t in hitl_tasks.values() if t.status != "archived"]
    if status:
        tasks = [t for t in tasks if t.status == status]
    return tasks


@app.patch("/api/v1/hitl/tasks/{task_id}")
async def update_task_status(task_id: str, update: dict):
    """Обновить статус и результат задачи"""
    task = hitl_tasks.get(task_id)
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Task not found")
    
    if "status" in update:
        task.status = update["status"]
    if "result" in update:
        task.result = update["result"]
    
    logger.info(f"Task {task_id} updated: {update}")
    return task

@app.get("/api/v1/tasks")
async def get_all_tasks(status: Optional[str] = None):
    """Получить все задачи (простой эндпоинт без HITL обёртки)"""
    tasks_list = []
    for task_id, task in hitl_tasks.items():
        if status and task.status != status:
            continue
        tasks_list.append({
            "id": task.id,
            "title": task.title,
            "description": task.description,
            "status": task.status,
            "created_at": task.created_at.isoformat() if task.created_at else None,
            "assigned_to": task.assigned_to,
            "result": task.result
        })
    return {"tasks": tasks_list, "total": len(tasks_list)}

@app.get("/api/v1/hitl/stats")
async def get_stats():
    """Статистика по задачам"""
    tasks = list(hitl_tasks.values())
    return {
        "total": len(tasks),
        "pending": len([t for t in tasks if t.status == "pending"]),
        "ready_for_review": len([t for t in tasks if t.status == "ready_for_review"]),
        "approved": len([t for t in tasks if t.status == "approved"]),
        "completed": len([t for t in tasks if t.status == "completed"]),
        "failed": len([t for t in tasks if t.status == "failed"])
    }

app.include_router(process_router)

if __name__ == "__main__":

async def execute_approved_task(task_id: str, task, agent_id: str):
    """Фоновое выполнение approve без блокировки ответа"""

    # Задержка чтобы UI успел показать статус approved
    await asyncio.sleep(2)

    try:
        # Шаг 1: CodeCraft генерирует код
        agent_map = {
            "codecraft_1": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/codecraft/agent.py",
            "coder": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/codecraft/agent.py",
            "coder_1": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/codecraft/agent.py",
            "architect_1": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/architect/agent.py",
            "architect": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/architect/agent.py",
        }
        
        agent_path_str = agent_map.get(agent_id)
        if agent_path_str and Path(agent_path_str).exists():
            import importlib.util
            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(f"{agent_id}_agent", agent_path_str)
            agent_module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
            spec.loader.exec_module(agent_module)
            
            agent_class = None
            for attr_name in dir(agent_module):
                if attr_name.endswith("Agent"):
                    agent_class = getattr(agent_module, attr_name)
                    break
            
            if agent_class:
                agent = agent_class()
                payload = {
                    "task": task.description,
                    "target_file": task.data.get("target_file", ""),
                    "existing_content": task.data.get("existing_content", "")
                }
                result = agent.execute(payload) if hasattr(agent, 'execute') else agent.execute(task.description, payload)
                task.result = result
                logger.info(f"Task {task_id}: CodeCraft completed")
        
        # Шаг 2: Integrator применяет изменения
        if task.result and task.result.get("pr"):
            integrator_path = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/integrator/agent.py"
            if Path(integrator_path).exists():
                spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("integrator_agent", integrator_path)
                integrator_module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
                spec.loader.exec_module(integrator_module)
                
                integrator = integrator_module.IntegratorAgent()
                integrator_payload = {
                    "task_id": task_id,
                    "pr": task.result["pr"]
                }
                integrator_result = integrator.execute(integrator_payload)
                task.result["integration"] = integrator_result
                
                if integrator_result.get("status") == "success":
                    task.status = "completed"
                    logger.info(f"Task {task_id}: Integration successful - {integrator_result.get('target_file')}")
                else:
                    task.status = "failed"
                    task.result["error"] = integrator_result.get("error")
                    logger.error(f"Task {task_id}: Integration failed - {integrator_result.get('error')}")
            else:
                task.status = "completed"
                logger.info(f"Task {task_id}: Completed without integration")
        else:
            task.status = "completed"
            logger.info(f"Task {task_id}: Completed (no PR to integrate)")
            
    except Exception as e:
        task.status = "failed"
        task.result = {"error": str(e)}
        logger.error(f"Task {task_id}: Failed - {e}")


@app.post("/api/v1/hitl/tasks/{task_id}/approve")
async def approve_hitl_task(task_id: str, approval: dict):
    """Одобрить HITL задачу и запустить выполнение в фоне"""
    task = None
    for t in hitl_tasks.values():
        if t.id == task_id:
            task = t
            break
    
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Task not found")
    
    if task.status not in ["pending", "ready_for_review"]:
        raise HTTPException(400, f"Task already {task.status}")
    
    # Меняем статус и запускаем фоновое выполнение
    task.status = "approved"
    task.approved_by = approval.get("approved_by", "human")
    
    agent_id = task.data.get("agent_id", "codecraft_1")
    
    # Запускаем асинхронное выполнение
    import asyncio
    asyncio.create_task(execute_approved_task(task_id, task, agent_id))
    
    logger.info(f"Task {task_id}: Approved, executing in background")
    
    return {
        "status": "approved", 
        "task_id": task_id, 
        "message": "Task approved, executing in background"
    }


# Маршрутизация задач по скилам агентов
async def route_task_by_skills(task_description: str) -> dict:
    """Анализирует задачу и выбирает подходящего агента по скилам"""
    task_lower = task_description.lower()
    
    # Скилы агентов
    skills_map = {
        "explorer_1": ["graph_analysis", "search", "find", "анализ", "поиск", "связи", "explore"],
        "architect_1": ["design", "diagram", "архитектура", "схема", "plantuml", "mermaid", "диаграмма", "последовательн", "sequence", "flowchart", "архитект"],
        "codecraft_1": ["code", "generate", "refactor", "код", "функция", "исправить", "написать", "скрипт", "python", "программ"],
        "general_1": ["chat", "help", "общий", "координация"]
    }
    
    scores = {}
    for agent_id, keywords in skills_map.items():
        score = sum(2 if kw in task_lower else 0 for kw in keywords)
        scores[agent_id] = score
    
    # Выбираем агента с максимальным скором
    best_agent = max(scores, key=scores.get)
    best_score = scores[best_agent]
    
    if best_score == 0:
        best_agent = "general_1"
    
    return {
        "agent_id": best_agent,
        "confidence": best_score,
        "reasoning": f"Matched {best_score} keywords"
    }

@app.post("/api/v1/hitl/tasks/route")
async def create_and_route_task(task_data: dict):
    """Создать задачу и автоматически назначить агента по скилам"""
    # Маршрутизация
    route = await route_task_by_skills(task_data.get("description", ""))
    
    # Создаем задачу с назначенным агентом
    # Проверить, есть ли ID в данных
    task_id = task_data.get('id') if isinstance(task_data, dict) else None
    if not task_id:
        task_id = f"task_{len(hitl_tasks) + 1}"
    task = HITLTask(
        id=task_id,
        title=task_data.get("title"),
        description=task_data.get("description"),
        status="assigned",
        created_at=datetime.now(),
        assigned_to=route["agent_id"],
        data=task_data.get("data", {}),
        action=task_data.get("action", "analyze")
    )
    hitl_tasks[task_id] = task
    
    # Запускаем выполнение агентом (в фоне)
    import asyncio
    asyncio.create_task(execute_agent_task(task_id, route["agent_id"], task_data.get("description")))
    
    return {"task_id": task_id, "assigned_to": route["agent_id"], "status": "assigned"}


@observe_agent("execute_agent_task")
async def execute_agent_task(task_id: str, agent_id: str, task_description: str):
    start_time = time.time()
    log_task(task_id, "agent_start", 0, {"agent": agent_id, "action": hitl_tasks[task_id].action})

    """Выполнение задачи агентом в песочнице с реальной генерацией"""
    hitl_tasks[task_id].status = "testing"
    
    import subprocess
    import json
    
    # Формируем задание для агента в зависимости от action
    action = hitl_tasks[task_id].action
    task_data = hitl_tasks[task_id].data or {}
    
    if action == "generate_config":
        config_type = task_data.get("config_type", "unknown")
        features = task_data.get("features", [])
        
        # Генерируем конфиг
        config_content = {
            "config_type": config_type,
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "features": features,
            "generated_by": agent_id,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "description": task_description
        }
        
        # Сохраняем в песочницу
        config_file = f"/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/temp/{config_type}_config.json"
        with open(config_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(config_content, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        
        hitl_tasks[task_id].result = {
            "config_file": config_file,
            "config_content": config_content,
            "test_status": "passed",
            "message": f"Конфиг {config_type} создан"
        }
    
    elif action == "analyze":
        query = task_data.get("query", task_description)
        
        # Извлекаем команду из описания (если есть)
        import subprocess
        import os
        from pathlib import Path
        
        # Парсим запрос: ищем путь для анализа
        import re
        path_match = re.search(r'/(Users|home|etc|var|tmp)[^\s]*', task_description)
        if path_match:
            target_path = path_match.group()
        else:
            target_path = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/temp"
        
        # Выполняем реальную команду ls -la через песочницу
        cmd = f'ls -la {target_path}'
        sandbox_cmd = f'/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/venv/bin/python /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/safe_executor_v4.py "analyzer" "{cmd}"'
        
        result = subprocess.run(sandbox_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
        
        # Парсим вывод ls -la
        files_list = []
        if result.returncode == 0:
            lines = result.stdout.strip().split('\n')
            for line in lines[1:]:  # пропускаем "total X"
                if line.strip():
                    parts = line.split()
                    if len(parts) >= 9:
                        files_list.append({
                            "permissions": parts[0],
                            "links": parts[1],
                            "owner": parts[2],
                            "group": parts[3],
                            "size": parts[4],
                            "month": parts[5],
                            "day": parts[6],
                            "time": parts[7],
                            "name": ' '.join(parts[8:])
                        })
        
        analysis = {
            "query": query,
            "analyzed_path": target_path,
            "command": cmd,
            "command_output": result.stdout[:2000],
            "command_error": result.stderr[:500],
            "files_count": len(files_list),
            "files": files_list[:50],  # первые 50 файлов
            "suggested_agents": ["explorer_1", "coder_1"],
            "confidence": 0.95
        }
        
        result_file = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/temp/analysis_result.json"
        with open(result_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(analysis, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        
        hitl_tasks[task_id].result = {
            "analysis_file": result_file,
            "analysis": analysis,
            "test_status": "passed" if result.returncode == 0 else "failed",
            "message": f"Проанализировано {len(files_list)} файлов в {target_path}"
        }
    
    elif action == "apply_patch" and task_data.get("patch"):
        # Применяем патч
        patch = task_data["patch"]
        applied = []
        from pathlib import Path
        PROJECT_ROOT = Path("/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub")
        
        for file_path, content in patch.items():
            target = PROJECT_ROOT / file_path
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')
            applied.append(file_path)
        
        hitl_tasks[task_id].result = {
            "applied": applied,
            "test_status": "passed",
            "message": f"Применено {len(applied)} файлов"
        }
    

    elif action == "generate_code":
        agent_type = "coder"
        method = "rule"
        language = task_data.get("language", "python")
        coding_task = task_data.get("task", task_description)
        
        # Используем LLM для генерации кода
        from tools.ollama_client import ollama_chat
        
        prompt = f"""
Ты профессиональный программист. Напиши код на {language} для следующей задачи.

Задача: {coding_task}

Требования:
- Код должен быть готов к использованию
- Добавь комментарии
- Включи пример использования

Ответь только кодом, без лишних пояснений.
"""
        
        generated_code = ollama_chat(prompt, model="llama3.1:8b")
        
        # Сохраняем код в файл
        code_file = f"/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/temp/generated_code_{task_id}.{language}"
        with open(code_file, "w") as f:
            f.write(generated_code)
        
        hitl_tasks[task_id].result = {
            "code_file": code_file,
            "code": generated_code,
            "language": language,
            "test_status": "passed",
            "message": f"Сгенерирован код на {language}"
        }
        
        hitl_tasks[task_id].status = "ready_for_review"
        logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completed by agent, ready for review")
    else:
        # Для других действий - простой echo
        result = subprocess.run(
            f'/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/venv/bin/python /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/safe_executor_v4.py "{agent_id}" "echo Выполняется: {task_description}"',
            shell=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True
        )
        
        hitl_tasks[task_id].result = {
            "agent_output": result.stdout,
            "agent_error": result.stderr,
            "test_status": "passed"
        }
    
    hitl_tasks[task_id].status = "ready_for_review"
    logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completed by {agent_id}, ready for review")

async def integrate_task(task_id: Optional[str] = None):
    """Интегратор применяет изменения в проект"""
    task = hitl_tasks[task_id]
    task.status = "integrating"
    
    # Проверяем, есть ли результат с config_file
    if task.result and task.result.get("config_file"):
        import shutil
        from pathlib import Path
        
        source = Path(task.result["config_file"])
        # Целевая директория в проекте
        target_dir = Path("/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/config/agents")
        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Копируем файл в проект
        target = target_dir / source.name
        shutil.copy2(source, target)
        
        task.status = "completed"
        task.result["integrated_to"] = str(target)
        task.result["message"] = f"Файл {source.name} скопирован в проект"
        logger.info(f"Task {task_id} integrated: {source} -> {target}")
    
    elif task.result and task.result.get("applied"):
        task.status = "completed"
        task.result["message"] = "Патч применен"
    
    else:
        task.status = "completed"
        task.result = task.result or {}
        task.result["message"] = "Задача завершена без интеграции"
    
    logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completed")

async def get_task(task_id: Optional[str] = None):
    """Получить задачу по ID"""
    task = hitl_tasks.get(task_id)
    if not task:

@app.post("/api/v1/hitl/tasks/composite")
async def create_composite_task(task_data: dict):
    """Создать составную задачу с несколькими шагами"""
    steps = task_data.get("steps", [])
    task_id = f"composite_{len(hitl_tasks) + 1}"
    
    # Создаем родительскую задачу
    parent_task = HITLTask(
        id=task_id,
        title=task_data.get("title"),
        description=task_data.get("description"),
        status="pending",
        created_at=datetime.now(),
        data={"steps": steps, "current_step": 0, "results": []}
    )
    hitl_tasks[task_id] = parent_task
    
    # Запускаем первый шаг
    import asyncio
    asyncio.create_task(execute_composite_step(task_id, 0))
    
    return {"task_id": task_id, "total_steps": len(steps), "status": "started"}

async def execute_composite_step(task_id: str, step_idx: int):
    """Выполнить шаг составной задачи"""
    task = hitl_tasks[task_id]
    steps = task.data.get("steps", [])
    
    if step_idx >= len(steps):
        task.status = "completed"
        task.result = {"message": "All steps completed", "results": task.data.get("results", [])}
        return
    
    step = steps[step_idx]
    task.status = "testing"
    task.data["current_step"] = step_idx
    
    # Выполняем действие в зависимости от типа шага
    if step.get("action") == "analyze":
        # Анализируем директорию
        import subprocess
        target_path = step.get("path", "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/components")
        cmd = f'ls -la {target_path}'
        sandbox_cmd = f'/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/venv/bin/python /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/safe_executor_v4.py "composite" "{cmd}"'
        result = subprocess.run(sandbox_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
        
        step_result = {
            "step": step_idx + 1,
            "action": "analyze",
            "path": target_path,
            "output": result.stdout[:2000],
            "status": "success" if result.returncode == 0 else "failed"
        }
    
    elif step.get("action") == "generate_config":
        # Генерируем конфиг
        import json
        config = {
            "config_type": step.get("config_type", "composite_config"),
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "features": step.get("features", []),
            "generated_by": "composite_agent",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        config_file = f"/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/sandbox/temp/composite_step_{step_idx}.json"
        with open(config_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(config, f, indent=2)
        
        step_result = {
            "step": step_idx + 1,
            "action": "generate_config",
            "config_file": config_file,
            "config": config,
            "status": "success"
        }
    
    else:
        step_result = {"step": step_idx + 1, "action": "unknown", "status": "skipped"}
    
    # Сохраняем результат шага
    results = task.data.get("results", [])
    results.append(step_result)
    task.data["results"] = results
    
    # Переходим к следующему шагу
    import asyncio
    asyncio.create_task(execute_composite_step(task_id, step_idx + 1))

# Импортируем ollama клиент
from tools.ollama_client import route_task_with_llm, analyze_with_llm

# Обновляем маршрутизатор с поддержкой LLM
async def smart_route_task(task_description: str) -> dict:
    """Умная маршрутизация: сначала правила, потом LLM"""
    task_lower = task_description.lower()
    
    # Быстрые правила (без LLM)
    if any(kw in task_lower for kw in ["граф", "связь", "node", "graph", "relationship"]):
        agent_type = "explorer"
        method = "rule"
    elif any(kw in task_lower for kw in ["архитектур", "диаграм", "design", "architecture"]):
        agent_type = "architect"
        method = "rule"
    elif any(kw in task_lower for kw in ["код", "функц", "code", "function", "refactor", "напиши", "написать", "функцию", "generate_code", "парсер", "класс", "асинхрон"]):
        agent_type = "coder"
        method = "rule"
    else:
        # Сложные задачи — используем LLM
        agent_type = route_task_with_llm(task_description)
        method = "llm"
    
    # Находим агента
    agent = next((a for a in orchestrator.agents.values() if a.type.value == agent_type), None)
    
    return {
        "agent_id": agent.id if agent else "general_1",
        "agent_type": agent_type,
        "confidence": 0.9 if method == "llm" else 0.7,
        "method": method,
        "task": task_description
    }

# ========== Kymatics: Декомпозиция задач ==========
from task_decomposer import decompose_task, create_subtasks_in_neo4j

class DecomposeRequest(BaseModel):
    task_id: Optional[str] = None
    title: str
    description: str

@app.post("/api/v1/tasks/decompose")
async def decompose_and_create_subtasks(request: DecomposeRequest):
    """Разложить задачу на подзадачи и сохранить в Neo4j"""
    # 1. Декомпозиция через LLM
    subtasks = await decompose_task(request.description, request.title)
    
    if not subtasks:
        raise HTTPException(500, "Failed to decompose task")
    
    # 2. Создаём подзадачи в Neo4j
    created = await create_subtasks_in_neo4j(request.task_id, subtasks)
    
    return {
        "parent_task_id": request.task_id,
        "subtasks_count": len(subtasks),
        "subtasks": subtasks,
        "created_ids": created
    }

@app.get("/api/v1/tasks/{task_id}/subtasks")
async def get_task_subtasks(task_id: Optional[str] = None):
    """Получить все подзадачи задачи из Neo4j"""
    import requests
    from neo4j_sync import NEO4J_URL, NEO4J_AUTH
    
    query = """
    MATCH (child:Task)-[:SUBTASK_OF]->(parent:Task {id: $task_id})
    RETURN child.id as id, child.title as title, child.status as status, child.complexity as complexity
    ORDER BY child.created_at
    """
    
    response = requests.post(
        NEO4J_URL,
        auth=NEO4J_AUTH,
        json={"statements": [{"statement": query, "parameters": {"task_id": task_id}}]}
    )
    
    result = response.json()
    subtasks = []
    for row in result.get("results", [{}])[0].get("data", []):
        subtasks.append({
            "id": row["row"][0],
            "title": row["row"][1],
            "status": row["row"][2],
            "complexity": row["row"][3]
        })
    
    return {"task_id": task_id, "subtasks": subtasks}

# ========== Knowledge Pool API ==========
# from knowledge_pool_faiss import get_knowledge_pool

@app.post("/api/v1/knowledge/add")
async def add_to_knowledge(task_id: str, title: str, description: str, code: str, success_rate: float = 1.0):
    """Добавить успешное решение в Knowledge Pool"""
    # kp = await get_knowledge_pool()
    result = await kp.add_solution(task_id, title, description, code, success_rate)
    return {"success": result, "task_id": task_id}

@app.post("/api/v1/knowledge/search")
async def search_knowledge(query: str, limit: int = 5):
    """Найти похожие решения"""
    # kp = await get_knowledge_pool()
    results = await kp.find_similar(query, limit)
    return {"query": query, "results": results, "count": len(results)}

# Регистрация командного роутера
from api_commands import router as commands_router
app.include_router(commands_router)


# Регистрация Command Executor Agent
from command_executor_agent import get_executor
app.state.command_executor = get_executor()

# Projects Router
from api_projects import router as projects_router
app.include_router(projects_router)

Теперь создадим тестовый шаблон.

## КОМАНДА: Создать тестовый шаблон через API

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/create \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "name": "telegram_collector",
    "description": "Собирает сообщения из Telegram каналов и сохраняет в файл",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "tags": ["telegram", "collector", "messaging"],
    "yaml": "template:\n  id: tg_collector_v1\n  name: Telegram Collector\n  description: Collects messages from Telegram channels\n  inputs:\n    - name: bot_token\n      type: string\n      required: true\n    - name: channels\n      type: list\n      required: true\n  steps:\n    - id: fetch_messages\n      type: http_node\n      config:\n        url: https://api.telegram.org/bot${bot_token}/getUpdates\n        method: GET\n    - id: save_to_file\n      type: file_node\n      config:\n        operation: write\n        path: ./telegram_messages.json\n        content: ${messages}\n  outputs:\n    - name: messages\n      type: string\n    - name: count\n      type: integer",
    "schedule": "0 */6 * * *"
  }'
```

**Затем проверим список шаблонов:**

```bash
curl http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/list
```

**И проверим скиллы:**

```bash
curl http://localhost:8010/api/v1/skills/list
```

**Покажите результаты.**

## 🎯 ДОСТИГНУТЫЕ РЕЗУЛЬТАТЫ

### API Endpoints (порт 8010):
- `GET /api/v1/templates/list` — список шаблонов
- `POST /api/v1/templates/create` — создание шаблона
- `GET /api/v1/templates/{id}` — получение шаблона
- `PUT /api/v1/templates/{id}` — обновление
- `DELETE /api/v1/templates/{id}` — удаление
- `POST /api/v1/templates/{id}/instantiate` — развернуть в процесс
- `GET /api/v1/skills/list` — список скиллов

### Зарегистрированные скиллы (10 штук):
1. http_get — HTTP GET запрос
2. http_post — HTTP POST запрос
3. bash_exec — выполнение bash команд
4. file_read — чтение файлов
5. file_write — запись файлов
6. json_parse — парсинг JSON
7. text_search — grep поиск
8. python_exec — выполнение Python кода
9. sleep — задержка
10. datetime — дата и время

import sys
import asyncio
import aiohttp
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any
from ..base_node import BaseNode
from ..registry import register_node

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent))

@register_node
class AgentNode(BaseNode):
    node_type = "agent"
    
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "agent_name": {"type": "string", "enum": ["architect", "coder", "validator", "smart_explorer", "llm"]},
            "task": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
            "model": {"type": "string", "default": "qwen2.5-coder:7b"},
            "temperature": {"type": "number", "default": 0.7},
            "max_tokens": {"type": "integer", "default": 2000},
            "system_prompt": {"type": "string", "default": ""},
            "timeout": {"type": "integer", "default": 60},
            "ollama_url": {"type": "string", "default": "http://localhost:11434"},
            "stream": {"type": "boolean", "default": True}
        },
        "required": ["task"]
    }
    
    async def send_log(self, execution_id: str, log_type: str, data: Dict):
        try:
            from api_processes import send_log
            await send_log(execution_id, log_type, data)
        except:
            pass
    
    async def execute(self, context: Dict, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        # ОТЛАДКА: вывести что пришло в config
        print(f"🔍 [AgentNode] DEBUG config: {config}")
        print(f"🔍 [AgentNode] DEBUG config keys: {config.keys()}")
        print(f"🔍 [AgentNode] DEBUG task value: {config.get('task')}")
        print(f"🔍 [AgentNode] DEBUG task type: {type(config.get('task'))}")
        
        agent_name = config.get("agent_name", "llm")
        task = config.get("task")
        timeout = config.get("timeout", 60)
        execution_id = context.get("execution_id")
        
        # Если task не строка, преобразуем
        if task is not None and not isinstance(task, str):
            task = str(task)
        
        if not task:
            return {"success": False, "error": "Task is required and cannot be empty"}
        
        task = self._substitute_vars(task, context.get("variables", {}))
        
        await self.send_log(execution_id, "agent_start", {
            "agent": agent_name,
            "task": task[:200]
        })
        
        print(f"🤖 [AgentNode] Запуск: {agent_name}, task: {task[:100]}")
        
        try:
            if agent_name == "llm":
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    self._call_llm(config, context, execution_id),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
            else:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    self._call_specialized_agent(agent_name, task, context),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
            
            await self.send_log(execution_id, "agent_complete", {
                "success": result.get("success", False),
                "summary": str(result.get("response", result.get("result", "")))[:200]
            })
            
            return result
            
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            await self.send_log(execution_id, "agent_error", {"error": f"Timeout after {timeout} seconds"})
            return {"success": False, "agent": agent_name, "error": f"Timeout after {timeout} seconds"}
        except Exception as e:
            await self.send_log(execution_id, "agent_error", {"error": str(e)})
            return {"success": False, "agent": agent_name, "error": str(e)}
    
    def _substitute_vars(self, text: str, variables: Dict) -> str:
        import re
        if not text:
            return ""
        def replace_var(match):
            var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
            return str(variables.get(var_name, match.group(0)))
        return re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replace_var, text)

"""
Process Designer Agent - Генерация процессов из текстовых требований с использованием LLM
с валидацией и итеративным исправлением
"""
import json
import httpx
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Tuple
from layout_engine import apply_layout


class ProcessValidator:
    """Валидатор BPMN процессов"""
    
    def validate(self, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
        errors = []
        
        # 1. Проверка наличия START и END
        start_nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'start' or n.get('type') == 'start']
        end_nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'end' or n.get('type') == 'end']
        
        if not start_nodes:
            errors.append("Process must have a START node")
        if not end_nodes:
            errors.append("Process must have at least one END node")
        
        # 2. START имеет ровно 1 исходящую связь
        if start_nodes:
            start_id = start_nodes[0]['id']
            start_outgoing = [e for e in edges if e.get('source') == start_id]
            if len(start_outgoing) != 1:
                errors.append(f"START node must have exactly 1 outgoing edge (found {len(start_outgoing)})")
        
        # 3. CONDITION имеет ровно 2 исходящих связи
        condition_nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'condition' or n.get('type') == 'condition']
        for cond in condition_nodes:
            outgoing = [e for e in edges if e.get('source') == cond['id']]
            if len(outgoing) != 2:
                errors.append(f"CONDITION node must have exactly 2 outgoing edges (found {len(outgoing)})")
        
        # 4. JOIN имеет минимум 2 входящих связи
        join_nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'join' or n.get('type') == 'join']
        for join in join_nodes:
            incoming = [e for e in edges if e.get('target') == join['id']]
            if len(incoming) < 2:
                errors.append(f"JOIN node must have at least 2 incoming edges (found {len(incoming)})")
        
        # 5. Нет agent → agent
        for edge in edges:
            source_type = next((n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') or n.get('type') for n in nodes if n['id'] == edge.get('source')), None)
            target_type = next((n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') or n.get('type') for n in nodes if n['id'] == edge.get('target')), None)
            
            if source_type == 'agent' and target_type == 'agent':
                errors.append(f"Invalid connection: agent cannot connect to agent ({edge['source']} → {edge['target']})")
        
        # 6. Проверка на недостижимые узлы
        if start_nodes:
            start_id = start_nodes[0]['id']
            reachable = set()
            queue = [start_id]
            while queue:
                current = queue.pop(0)
                if current in reachable:
                    continue
                reachable.add(current)
                outgoing = [e.get('target') for e in edges if e.get('source') == current]
                queue.extend(outgoing)
            
            unreachable = [n['id'] for n in nodes if n['id'] not in reachable]
            if unreachable:
                errors.append(f"Unreachable nodes: {', '.join(unreachable)}")
        
        return len(errors) == 0, errors

class ProcessAutoFixer:
    """Автоматическое исправление ошибок БЕЗ LLM"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def add_missing_start(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict]]:
        """Добавляет START узел"""
        start_exists = any(n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'start' or n.get('type') == 'start' for n in nodes)
        if not start_exists and nodes:
            first_agent = next((n for n in nodes if n.get('type') == 'agent'), nodes[0])
            start_node = {
                "id": "start",
                "type": "start", 
                "position": {"x": first_agent.get('position', {}).get('x', 100) - 250, "y": first_agent.get('position', {}).get('y', 200)},
                "data": {"label": "Start", "node_type": "start", "status": "pending"}
            }
            nodes.insert(0, start_node)
            edges.insert(0, {
                "id": "e_auto_start",
                "source": "start",
                "target": first_agent['id'],
                "sourceHandle": "right-1",
                "targetHandle": "left-1"
            })
        return nodes, edges
    
    @staticmethod
    def add_missing_ends(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict]]:
        """Добавляет END узлы"""
        end_exists = any(n.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'end' or n.get('type') == 'end' for n in nodes)
        if not end_exists and nodes:
            sources = {e.get('source') for e in edges}
            nodes_with_no_outgoing = [n for n in nodes if n['id'] not in sources and n.get('type') != 'end']
            for node in nodes_with_no_outgoing:
                end_node = {
                    "id": f"end_{node['id']}",
                    "type": "end",
                    "position": {"x": node.get('position', {}).get('x', 500) + 250, "y": node.get('position', {}).get('y', 200)},
                    "data": {"label": "End", "node_type": "end", "status": "pending"}
                }
                nodes.append(end_node)
                edges.append({
                    "id": f"e_auto_end_{node['id']}",
                    "source": node['id'],
                    "target": end_node['id'],
                    "sourceHandle": "right-1",
                    "targetHandle": "left-1"
                })
        return nodes, edges
    
    @staticmethod
    def auto_fix(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], errors: List[str]) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict], List[str]]:
        """Применяет авто-исправления, возвращает неисправленные ошибки"""
        remaining = errors.copy()
        
        # Исправляем отсутствие START
        if any("START" in e for e in errors):
            nodes, edges = ProcessAutoFixer.add_missing_start(nodes, edges)
            remaining = [e for e in remaining if "START" not in e]
        
        # Исправляем отсутствие END  
        if any("END" in e for e in errors):
            nodes, edges = ProcessAutoFixer.add_missing_ends(nodes, edges)
            remaining = [e for e in remaining if "END" not in e]
        
        return nodes, edges, remaining

class ProcessDesignerAgent:
    """Агент для проектирования процессов на основе бизнес-требований с использованием LLM"""
    
    def __init__(self, ollama_url: str = "http://localhost:11434"):
        self.ollama_url = ollama_url
        self.model = "qwen2.5-coder:7b"
        self.validator = ProcessValidator()
        self.max_iterations = 3
    
    async def design_process(self, requirement: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Генерирует структуру процесса из текстового требования через LLM с итеративным исправлением"""

    async def design_process(self, requirement: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Генерирует структуру процесса из текстового требования через LLM с итеративным исправлением"""
        print(f"🤖 Process Designer: Analyzing requirement: {requirement[:100]}...")
        
        # Начальная генерация
        llm_response = await self._call_llm(requirement)
        nodes, edges = self._parse_llm_response(llm_response)
        
        if not nodes:
            print("⚠️ LLM response empty, using fallback")
            nodes, edges = self._fallback_generate(requirement)
        
        # Итеративное исправление (ОПТИМИЗИРОВАННО)
        iteration = 0
        is_valid, errors = self.validator.validate(nodes, edges)
        
        # ШАГ 1: Авто-исправление без LLM
        if not is_valid:
            nodes, edges, remaining_errors = ProcessAutoFixer.auto_fix(nodes, edges, errors)
            is_valid, errors = self.validator.validate(nodes, edges)
            if remaining_errors and len(remaining_errors) < len(errors):
                print(f"🔧 Auto-fixed {len(errors) - len(remaining_errors)} errors, {len(remaining_errors)} critical")
                errors = remaining_errors
        
        # ШАГ 2: LLM исправление (только для критических, макс 1 раз)
        if not is_valid and iteration < 1:
            print(f"🔄 LLM fixing {len(errors)} critical errors")
            nodes, edges = await self._fix_errors(nodes, edges, errors, requirement)
            if nodes:
                is_valid, errors = self.validator.validate(nodes, edges)
                iteration += 1
        
        if is_valid:
            print(f"✅ Process is valid (auto-fix + {iteration} LLM call(s))")
        else:
            print(f"⚠️ Process still has {len(errors)} errors after optimization")
        
        # Нормализуем типы узлов
        for node in nodes:
            if node.get('type') == 'start' or node.get('type') == 'end':
                node['type'] = 'custom'
            if 'data' not in node:
                node['data'] = {}
            if 'status' not in node['data']:
                node['data']['status'] = 'pending'
            if 'node_type' not in node['data']:
                node['data']['node_type'] = node.get('type', 'agent')

        # Применяем автоматический расчет координат
        nodes = apply_layout(nodes, edges)                
        
        print(f"✅ Generated {len(nodes)} nodes and {len(edges)} edges")
        
        return {
            "nodes": nodes,
            "edges": edges,
            "yaml": "",
            "mermaid": "",
            "steps": []
        }
    
    async def _fix_errors(self, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], errors: List[str], requirement: str) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict]]:
        """Исправляет ошибки валидации"""
        
        prompt = f'''Ты - эксперт BPMN. Исправь ошибки в процессе.

## ТРЕБОВАНИЕ:
{requirement}

## ТЕКУЩАЯ СТРУКТУРА:
Nodes: {json.dumps(nodes, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)}
Edges: {json.dumps(edges, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)}

## ОШИБКИ ВАЛИДАЦИИ:
{chr(10).join(f'- {e}' for e in errors)}

## ПРАВИЛА BPMN:
1. START → единственный выход
2. END → нет выходов
3. CONDITION → ровно 2 выхода (right-1 и right-2)
4. JOIN → мин. 2 входа, 1 выход
5. PARALLEL → используется с JOIN
6. Не agent → agent
7. Все узлы должны быть достижимы из START

## ПРИМЕР ПРАВИЛЬНОЙ СТРУКТУРЫ:
{{
  "nodes": [
    {{"id": "start", "type": "start", "position": {{"x": 100, "y": 200}}, "data": {{"label": "Start", "node_type": "start"}}}},
    {{"id": "agent_1", "type": "agent", "position": {{"x": 350, "y": 200}}, "data": {{"label": "Обработка", "node_type": "agent", "agent_name": "coder"}}}},
    {{"id": "end", "type": "end", "position": {{"x": 600, "y": 200}}, "data": {{"label": "End", "node_type": "end"}}}}
  ],
  "edges": [
    {{"id": "e1", "source": "start", "target": "agent_1", "sourceHandle": "right-1", "targetHandle": "left-1"}},
    {{"id": "e2", "source": "agent_1", "target": "end", "sourceHandle": "right-1", "targetHandle": "left-1"}}
  ]
}}

Верни ТОЛЬКО JSON, без пояснений.
'''
        
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=90.0) as client:
                response = await client.post(
                    f"{self.ollama_url}/api/generate",
                    json={
                        "model": self.model,
                        "prompt": prompt,
                        "stream": False,
                        "temperature": 0.2,
                        "num_predict": 4000
                    }
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    result = response.json()
                    return self._parse_llm_response(result.get("response", ""))
                else:
                    print(f"⚠️ Fix error: {response.status_code}")
                    return nodes, edges
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Fix exception: {e}")
            return nodes, edges
    
    async def _call_llm(self, requirement: str) -> str:
        """Вызов Ollama для анализа требования с правилами BPMN"""
        
        prompt = f'''
Ты - архитектор BPMN процессов. Создай валидную структуру процесса по требованию.

## ТРЕБОВАНИЕ:
{requirement}

## ПРАВИЛА BPMN (ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНЫЕ):
1. КАЖДЫЙ процесс должен начинаться с START узла
2. КАЖДЫЙ процесс должен заканчиваться END узлом (минимум 1)
3. START НЕ может иметь входящих связей
4. END НЕ может иметь исходящих связей
5. CONDITION ДОЛЖЕН иметь ровно 2 исходящих связи (true/false)
6. JOIN ДОЛЖЕН иметь минимум 2 входящих связи
7. PARALLEL ДОЛЖЕН сходиться в JOIN
8. Нельзя соединять agent -> agent напрямую
9. START узел может иметь ТОЛЬКО ОДНУ исходящую связь
10. JOIN узел может иметь ТОЛЬКО ОДНУ исходящую связь

## ПРАВИЛА ХЭНДЛОВ:
1. sourceHandle: "right-1", "right-2"
2. targetHandle: "left-1"
3. Для condition: правый хэндл для true, правый-2 для false

## ДОСТУПНЫЕ ТИПЫ УЗЛОВ:
- start, end, agent, condition, parallel, join, approval, http_node, bash, git

## ФОРМАТ ОТВЕТА (ТОЛЬКО JSON):
{{
  "nodes": [
    {{"id": "start", "type": "start", "position": {{"x": 100, "y": 200}}, "data": {{"label": "Start", "node_type": "start"}}}},
    {{"id": "agent_1", "type": "agent", "position": {{"x": 350, "y": 200}}, "data": {{"label": "Обработка", "node_type": "agent", "agent_name": "coder"}}}},
    {{"id": "end", "type": "end", "position": {{"x": 600, "y": 200}}, "data": {{"label": "End", "node_type": "end"}}}}
  ],
  "edges": [
    {{"id": "e1", "source": "start", "target": "agent_1", "sourceHandle": "right-1", "targetHandle": "left-1"}},
    {{"id": "e2", "source": "agent_1", "target": "end", "sourceHandle": "right-1", "targetHandle": "left-1"}}
  ]
}}

## ВАЖНО:
- Верни ТОЛЬКО JSON, без пояснений
- START и END должны быть всегда
- Координаты: x увеличивается на 250-300, y разносит ветки на 150-200

Создай процесс по требованию.
'''
        
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=90.0) as client:
                response = await client.post(
                    f"{self.ollama_url}/api/generate",
                    json={
                        "model": self.model,
                        "prompt": prompt,
                        "stream": False,
                        "temperature": 0.3,
                        "num_predict": 4000
                    }
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    result = response.json()
                    return result.get("response", "")
                else:
                    print(f"⚠️ Ollama error: {response.status_code}")
                    return ""
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Ollama exception: {e}")
            return ""
    
    def _parse_llm_response(self, llm_response: str) -> tuple:
        """Парсинг JSON ответа от LLM в формат nodes/edges"""
        if not llm_response:
            return [], []

## КОМАНДА: Добавить автоматическое заполнение task в api_processes.py

```bash
nano /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_processes.py
```

**Найдите функцию `save_process` и добавьте перед сохранением:**

```python
@router.post("/save")
async def save_process(request: SaveRequest):
    """Сохранение процесса в БД с поддержкой project_id"""
    try:
        # P3 FIX: Автоматически добавляем task для агентов, если отсутствует
        for node in request.nodes:
            if node.get('type') == 'agent' or node.get('data', {}).get('node_type') == 'agent':
                node_data = node.get('data', {})
                if 'task' not in node_data or not node_data.get('task'):
                    # Генерируем task из label или имени узла
                    label = node_data.get('label', node.get('id', 'agent'))
                    node_data['task'] = f"Выполни действие: {label}"
                    node['data'] = node_data
                    logger.info(f"Added missing task for agent node {node.get('id')}: {node_data['task']}")
        
        process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        db = get_process_db()
        
        success = db.save_process(
            process_id=process_id,
            name=request.name,
            description=request.description,
            nodes=request.nodes,
            edges=request.edges,
            yaml=request.yaml,
            mermaid=request.mermaid,
            variables=request.variables,
            project_id=request.project_id,
            project_path=request.project_path
        )
        
        if success:
            return {"status": "saved", "id": process_id, "project_id": request.project_id}
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to save process")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Save error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
```

"""
API endpoints for Process Designer with Project integration
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
from nodes import NodeRegistry
from process_executor import real_execution, get_execution_statuses
from websocket_logger import active_websockets, send_log, set_global_websockets

from websocket_logger import active_websockets, send_log

import uuid
import asyncio
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any

from process_designer_agent import get_process_designer
from process_models import get_process_db
from process_manager import get_manager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/process", tags=["process"])

class DesignRequest(BaseModel):
    requirement: str

class DesignResponse(BaseModel):
    nodes: list
    edges: list
    yaml: str
    mermaid: str
    steps: list

class SaveRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    description: str
    nodes: List[Dict]
    edges: List[Dict]
    yaml: str
    mermaid: str
    variables: Optional[Dict] = None
    project_id: Optional[str] = None
    project_path: Optional[str] = None


def normalize_handles(edges: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
    """Универсальное исправление хэндлов от LLM."""
    for edge in edges:
        source = edge.get('source')
        target = edge.get('target')

        # --- Исправляем sourceHandle (откуда идет стрелка) ---
        source_handle = edge.get('sourceHandle', 'right-1')
        # Приводим к нижнему регистру для поиска
        source_key = source_handle.lower()
        # Маппим все варианты на стандартный 'right-1'
        if any(x in source_key for x in ['right', 'bottom', 'top', '2', '3']):
            edge['sourceHandle'] = 'right-1'
        else:
            # Если не удалось определить, ставим по умолчанию
            edge['sourceHandle'] = 'right-1'

        # --- Исправляем targetHandle (куда идет стрелка) ---
        target_handle = edge.get('targetHandle', 'left-1')
        # Приводим к нижнему регистру для поиска
        target_key = target_handle.lower()
        # Маппим все варианты на стандартный 'left-1'
        if any(x in target_key for x in ['left', 'bottom', 'top', '2', '3']):
            edge['targetHandle'] = 'left-1'
        else:
            # Если не удалось определить, ставим по умолчанию
            edge['targetHandle'] = 'left-1'

        # Пропускаем петли
        if source == target:
            continue

    return edges


@router.post("/design", response_model=DesignResponse)
async def design_process(request: DesignRequest):
    """Генерация процесса из текстового требования"""
    try:
        designer = get_process_designer()
        result = await designer.design_process(request.requirement)
        
        # Нормализуем хэндлы
        if 'edges' in result and result['edges']:
            result['edges'] = normalize_handles(result['edges'])
            logger.info(f"Normalized {len(result['edges'])} edges")
        
        # Убедимся, что у всех узлов есть type 'custom'
        if 'nodes' in result:
            for node in result['nodes']:
                if node.get('type') != 'custom':
                    node['type'] = 'custom'
        
        return DesignResponse(**result)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Design error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.post("/save")
async def save_process(request: SaveRequest):
    """Сохранение процесса в БД с поддержкой project_id"""
    try:
        process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        db = get_process_db()
        
        success = db.save_process(
            process_id=process_id,
            name=request.name,
            description=request.description,
            nodes=request.nodes,
            edges=request.edges,
            yaml=request.yaml,
            mermaid=request.mermaid,
            variables=request.variables,
            project_id=request.project_id,
            project_path=request.project_path
        )
        
        if success:
            return {"status": "saved", "id": process_id, "project_id": request.project_id}
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to save process")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Save error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/node-types")
async def get_node_types():
    """Получить список всех доступных типов узлов"""
    return {
        "node_types": NodeRegistry.list_all()
    }


@router.get("/list")
async def list_processes(project_id: Optional[str] = None):
    """Список всех процессов, опционально фильтруя по project_id"""
    try:
        db = get_process_db()
        if project_id:
            processes = db.get_processes_by_project(project_id)
        else:
            processes = db.list_processes()
        return {"processes": processes, "filter": {"project_id": project_id}}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"List error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/list/by-project/{project_id}")
async def list_processes_by_project(project_id: str):
    """Получить все процессы для конкретного проекта"""
    try:
        db = get_process_db()
        processes = db.get_processes_by_project(project_id)
        return {"success": True, "processes": processes, "project_id": project_id}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"List by project error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.get("/{process_id}")
async def get_process(process_id: str):
    """Получить процесс по ID"""
    try:
        db = get_process_db()
        process = db.get_process(process_id)
        if not process:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Process not found")
        return process
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Get process error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


@router.delete("/{process_id}")
async def delete_process(process_id: str):
    """Удалить процесс по ID"""
    try:
        db = get_process_db()
        success = db.delete_process(process_id)
        if success:
            return {"success": True, "message": f"Process {process_id} deleted"}
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Process not found or could not be deleted")
    except HTTPException:
        raise

        <div className="k-logo">

          <Link to="/history" className="k-nav-item" activeProps={{ className: 'active' }}>

          </Link>

            Projects
          </Link>
          <Link to="/templates" className="k-nav-item" activeProps={{ className: 'active' }}>
            Templates
          </Link>
        </div>
      </nav>

}

/* eslint-disable */
// @ts-nocheck

import { Route as rootRouteImport } from './routes/__root'
import { Route as ProcessDesignerRouteImport } from './routes/process-designer'
import { Route as ProjectsRouteImport } from './routes/projects'
import { Route as IndexRouteImport } from './routes/index'
import { Route as HitlIndexRouteImport } from './routes/hitl/index'

const ProcessDesignerRoute = ProcessDesignerRouteImport.update({
  id: '/process-designer',
  path: '/process-designer',
  getParentRoute: () => rootRouteImport,
} as any)

const ProjectsRoute = ProjectsRouteImport.update({
  id: '/projects',
  path: '/projects',
  getParentRoute: () => rootRouteImport,
} as any)

const IndexRoute = IndexRouteImport.update({
  id: '/',
  path: '/',
  getParentRoute: () => rootRouteImport,
} as any)

const HitlIndexRoute = HitlIndexRouteImport.update({
  id: '/hitl/',
  path: '/hitl/',
  getParentRoute: () => rootRouteImport,
} as any)

export const routeTree = rootRouteImport.addChildren([
  IndexRoute,
  ProcessDesignerRoute,
  ProjectsRoute,
  HitlIndexRoute,
])

export interface FileRoutesByFullPath {
  '/': typeof IndexRoute
  '/process-designer': typeof ProcessDesignerRoute
  '/projects': typeof ProjectsRoute
  '/hitl/': typeof HitlIndexRoute
}

export interface FileRoutesByTo {
  '/': typeof IndexRoute
  '/process-designer': typeof ProcessDesignerRoute
  '/projects': typeof ProjectsRoute
  '/hitl': typeof HitlIndexRoute
}

export interface FileRoutesById {
  __root__: typeof rootRouteImport
  '/': typeof IndexRoute
  '/process-designer': typeof ProcessDesignerRoute
  '/projects': typeof ProjectsRoute
  '/hitl/': typeof HitlIndexRoute
}

export interface FileRouteTypes {
  fileRoutesByFullPath: FileRoutesByFullPath
  fullPaths: '/' | '/process-designer' | '/projects' | '/hitl/'
}

## КОМАНДА 22: Добавить Templates в App.tsx

```bash
nano /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/App.tsx
```

**Замените содержимое на:**

```typescript
import { Routes, Route, useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Header } from './components/layout/Header';
import { Navigation } from './components/layout/Navigation';
import { TerminalPage } from './pages/TerminalPage';
import { TasksPage } from './pages/TasksPage';
import { HistoryPage } from './pages/HistoryPage';
import { GraphPage } from './pages/GraphPage';
import { AgentsPage } from './pages/AgentsPage';
import ProcessDesignerPage from './features/process-designer/ProcessDesignerPage';
import { TemplateList } from './features/templates/TemplateList';

type TabId = 'terminal' | 'tasks' | 'history' | 'graph' | 'agents' | 'process-designer' | 'templates' | 'projects';

function App() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  
  const getActiveTabFromPath = (pathname: string): TabId => {
    if (pathname === '/tasks') return 'tasks';
    if (pathname === '/history') return 'history';
    if (pathname === '/graph') return 'graph';
    if (pathname === '/agents') return 'agents';
    if (pathname === '/process-designer') return 'process-designer';
    if (pathname === '/templates') return 'templates';
    if (pathname === '/projects') return 'projects';
    return 'terminal';
  };
  
  const activeTab = getActiveTabFromPath(location.pathname);
  
  const handleTabChange = (tab: TabId) => {
    if (tab === 'terminal') {
      navigate('/');
    } else {
      navigate(`/${tab}`);
    }
  };
  
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100vh' }}>
      <Header />
      <Navigation activeTab={activeTab} onTabChange={handleTabChange} />
      <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<TerminalPage />} />
          <Route path="/tasks" element={<TasksPage />} />
          <Route path="/history" element={<HistoryPage />} />
          <Route path="/graph" element={<GraphPage />} />
          <Route path="/agents" element={<AgentsPage />} />
          <Route path="/process-designer" element={<ProcessDesignerPage />} />
          <Route path="/templates" element={<TemplateList />} />
        </Routes>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

**Сохраните файл (Ctrl+O, Enter, Ctrl+X).**
