import { Routes, Route, useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Header } from './components/layout/Header';
import { Navigation } from './components/layout/Navigation';
import { TerminalPage } from './pages/TerminalPage';
import { TasksPage } from './pages/TasksPage';
import { HistoryPage } from './pages/HistoryPage';
import { GraphPage } from './pages/GraphPage';
import { AgentsPage } from './pages/AgentsPage';
import ProcessDesignerPage from './features/process-designer/ProcessDesignerPage';

type TabId = 'terminal' | 'tasks' | 'history' | 'graph' | 'agents' | 'process-designer';

function App() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  
  const getActiveTabFromPath = (pathname: string): TabId => {
    if (pathname === '/tasks') return 'tasks';
    if (pathname === '/history') return 'history';
    if (pathname === '/graph') return 'graph';
    if (pathname === '/agents') return 'agents';
    if (pathname === '/process-designer') return 'process-designer';
    return 'terminal';
  };
  
  const activeTab = getActiveTabFromPath(location.pathname);
  
  const handleTabChange = (tab: TabId) => {
    if (tab === 'terminal') {
      navigate('/');
    } else {
      navigate(`/${tab}`);
    }
  };
  
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100vh' }}>
      <Header />
      <Navigation activeTab={activeTab} onTabChange={handleTabChange} />
      <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<TerminalPage />} />
          <Route path="/tasks" element={<TasksPage />} />
          <Route path="/history" element={<HistoryPage />} />
          <Route path="/graph" element={<GraphPage />} />
          <Route path="/agents" element={<AgentsPage />} />
          <Route path="/process-designer" element={<ProcessDesignerPage />} />
        </Routes>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

## КОМАНДА 29: Создать упрощенную версию TemplateList для теста

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/templates/TemplateListSimple.tsx << 'EOF'
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export const TemplateListSimple = () => {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/list')
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => {
        setTemplates(data.templates || []);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(err => {
        setError(err.message);
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  if (loading) return <div>Loading templates...</div>;
  if (error) return <div>Error: {error}</div>;
  if (templates.length === 0) return <div>No templates found</div>;

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
      <h2>Templates Library</h2>
      <ul>
        {templates.map(t => (
          <li key={t.id}>
            <strong>{t.name}</strong> - {t.description}
            <br />
            <small>Tags: {t.tags?.join(', ')} | Schedule: {t.schedule || 'none'}</small>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
};
EOF
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Вот проблема! Фронтенд использует **TanStack Router** (`RouterProvider`), а не `react-router-dom`. Наш App.tsx не используется. Все маршруты определяются в `routeTree.gen.ts`, где нет `/templates`.

Нужно добавить `/templates` в TanStack Router.

## КОМАНДА 43: Добавить маршрут templates в TanStack Router

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/routes
cat > templates.tsx << 'EOF'
import { createFileRoute } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { TemplateList } from '../features/templates/TemplateList';

export const Route = createFileRoute('/templates')({
  component: TemplateList,
});
EOF
```

## КОМАНДА 44: Перегенерировать routeTree

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3
rm -f src/routeTree.gen.ts
npm run dev
```

**После запуска нажмите `Ctrl+C` и затем:**


## 👤 **Kirill**

kirillkravcov@MacBook-Air UniversalCreativeHub % cat /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/main.tsx
import React from 'react'
import './shared/services/eventBus';
import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client'
import { RouterProvider, createRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { routeTree } from './routeTree.gen'
import { Kernel } from './core/Kernel'
import { ProcessManager } from './managers/ProcessManager'
import { AgentManager } from './managers/AgentManager'
import { HitlManager } from './managers/HitlManager'
import { EventBus } from './core/EventBus'
import { Orchestrator } from './core/Orchestrator'
import './index.css'

// Создаем QueryClient для React Query
const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5, // 5 минут
      retry: 1,
    },
  },
})

async function initPlatform() {
  console.log('[Kernel] Initializing platform...')
  
  const processManager = ProcessManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  const agentManager = AgentManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  const hitlManager = HitlManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  
  Kernel.register(processManager)
  Kernel.register(agentManager)
  Kernel.register(hitlManager)
  
  await Kernel.init()
  console.log('[Kernel] All managers initialized')
  
  await processManager.start()
  await agentManager.start()
  await hitlManager.start()
  
  console.log('[Kernel] Statuses:', {
    process: processManager.getStatus(),
    agent: agentManager.getStatus(),
    hitl: hitlManager.getStatus(),
  })
}

const router = createRouter({ routeTree })

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register {
    router: typeof router
  }
}

declare global {
  interface Window {
    __KYMATICS__: any
  }
}

initPlatform().then(() => {
  window.__KYMATICS__ = { Kernel, EventBus, Orchestrator }
  console.log('[Kernel] Platform ready. Access window.__KYMATICS__')
  
  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <React.StrictMode>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </React.StrictMode>,
  )
}).catch(error => {
  console.error('[Kernel] Failed:', error)
  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <React.StrictMode>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <div style={{ padding: 20, color: 'red' }}>
          <h1>Platform Error</h1>
          <pre>{String(error)}</pre>
        </div>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </React.StrictMode>,
  )
})

```bash
/* eslint-disable */
// @ts-nocheck

import { Route as rootRoute } from './routes/__root'
import { Route as TemplatesRoute } from './routes/templates'
import { Route as ProcessDesignerRoute } from './routes/process-designer'
import { Route as ProjectsRoute } from './routes/projects'

export const routeTree = rootRoute.addChildren([
  IndexRoute,
  ProcessDesignerRoute,
  ProjectsRoute,
  TemplatesRoute,
])

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register {
    routeTree: typeof routeTree
  }
}
EOF
```

```typescript
import React from 'react'
import './shared/services/eventBus';
import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client'
import { BrowserRouter } from 'react-router-dom'
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import App from './App'
import { Kernel } from './core/Kernel'
import { ProcessManager } from './managers/ProcessManager'
import { AgentManager } from './managers/AgentManager'
import { HitlManager } from './managers/HitlManager'
import { EventBus } from './core/EventBus'
import { Orchestrator } from './core/Orchestrator'
import './index.css'

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5,
      retry: 1,
    },
  },
})

async function initPlatform() {
  console.log('[Kernel] Initializing platform...')
  
  const processManager = ProcessManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  const agentManager = AgentManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  const hitlManager = HitlManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  
  Kernel.register(processManager)
  Kernel.register(agentManager)
  Kernel.register(hitlManager)
  
  await Kernel.init()
  
  await processManager.start()
  await agentManager.start()
  await hitlManager.start()
}

initPlatform().then(() => {
  window.__KYMATICS__ = { Kernel, EventBus, Orchestrator }
  console.log('[Kernel] Platform ready. Access window.__KYMATICS__')
  
  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <React.StrictMode>

}).catch(error => {

      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <div style={{ padding: 20, color: 'red' }}>
          <h1>Platform Error</h1>
          <pre>{String(error)}</pre>
        </div>

})
```

```bash
import React from 'react'
import './shared/services/eventBus';
import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client'

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
```

import { ProcessManager } from './managers/ProcessManager'
import { AgentManager } from './managers/AgentManager'
import { HitlManager } from './managers/HitlManager'
import { EventBus } from './core/EventBus'
import { Orchestrator } from './core/Orchestrator'
import './index.css'

// Создаем QueryClient для React Query
const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5, // 5 минут
      retry: 1,
    },
  },
})

async function initPlatform() {
  console.log('[Kernel] Initializing platform...')
  
  const processManager = ProcessManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  const agentManager = AgentManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  const hitlManager = HitlManager.getInstance(EventBus, Orchestrator)
  
  Kernel.register(processManager)
  Kernel.register(agentManager)
  Kernel.register(hitlManager)
  
  await Kernel.init()
  console.log('[Kernel] All managers initialized')
  
  await processManager.start()
  await agentManager.start()
  await hitlManager.start()
  
  console.log('[Kernel] Statuses:', {
    process: processManager.getStatus(),
    agent: agentManager.getStatus(),
    hitl: hitlManager.getStatus(),
  })
}

const router = createRouter({ routeTree })

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register {
    router: typeof router
  }
}

declare global {
  interface Window {
    __KYMATICS__: any
  }
}

initPlatform().then(() => {
  window.__KYMATICS__ = { Kernel, EventBus, Orchestrator }
  console.log('[Kernel] Platform ready. Access window.__KYMATICS__')
  
  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <React.StrictMode>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </React.StrictMode>,
  )
}).catch(error => {
  console.error('[Kernel] Failed:', error)
  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <React.StrictMode>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <div style={{ padding: 20, color: 'red' }}>
          <h1>Platform Error</h1>
          <pre>{String(error)}</pre>
        </div>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </React.StrictMode>,
  )
})

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Эти ошибки в консоли — **не критические** и не влияют на работу. Они делятся на три типа:

## 1. **React props в нижнем регистре** (`justifyContent`, `alignItems`, `flexWrap`)
MUI (Material-UI) использует camelCase (`justifyContent`), но React ругается, что в DOM попадают эти атрибуты. Это **предупреждения**, не ошибки. Исправлять не обязательно, но можно заменить на стандартные CSS-свойства или использовать `sx`-проп.

## 2. **Небулево значение для `item`**
Атрибут `item="true"` ожидает строку или булево значение. Это тоже предупреждение от MUI Grid.

## 3. **`notFoundError` в TanStack Router**
Это предупреждение о том, что на маршрут не настроена страница 404. Не влияет на работу.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import '../process-designer/process-designer.css';

interface Template {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  version: string;
  tags: string[];
  schedule: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export const TemplateList = () => {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<Template[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchTag, setSearchTag] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTemplates();
  }, [searchTag]);

  const loadTemplates = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const url = searchTag 
        ? `http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/list?tag=${searchTag}`
        : 'http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/list';
      const response = await fetch(url);
      const data = await response.json();
      setTemplates(data.templates || []);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Failed to load templates');
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm('Delete this template?')) return;
    try {
      await fetch(`http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      await loadTemplates();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Delete failed:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleInstantiate = async (template: Template) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/${template.id}/instantiate`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({})
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      alert(`Process created: ${data.process_id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Failed to instantiate template');
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="process-designer-container" style={{ padding: '20px' }}>
        <div className="process-designer-loading">Loading templates...</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="process-designer-container">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="process-designer-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="process-designer-title">Template Library</h1>
          <p className="process-designer-subtitle">Reusable process templates</p>
        </div>
        <button className="process-designer-create-btn" onClick={() => alert('Create template modal')}>
          + New Template
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      <div style={{ marginBottom: '20px' }}>
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search by tag..."
          value={searchTag}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchTag(e.target.value)}
          style={{
            padding: '8px 12px',
            borderRadius: '6px',
            border: '1px solid var(--border-color, #e5e7eb)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary, #f9fafb)',
            width: '250px',
            fontSize: '14px'
          }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Error */}
      {error && (
        <div style={{ color: 'red', padding: '12px', background: '#fee2e2', borderRadius: '6px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Templates Grid */}
      {templates.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '60px 20px', color: '#6b7280' }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '48px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>📁</div>
          <h3>No templates</h3>
          <p>Create your first template or save an existing process as a template</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="process-grid">
          {templates.map((template) => (
            <div key={template.id} className="process-card">
              <div className="process-card-header">
                <div className="process-card-title">{template.name}</div>
                <div className="process-card-actions">
                  <button
                    className="process-card-btn"
                    onClick={() => handleInstantiate(template)}
                    title="Create process from template"
                  >
                    ▶
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className="process-card-btn process-card-btn-danger"
                    onClick={() => handleDelete(template.id)}
                    title="Delete template"
                  >
                    🗑
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useFileStore } from './store/fileStore';
import { useDemoFileSystemProvider } from './hooks/useFileSystemProvider';
import { FileTree } from './components/FileTree/FileTree';
import { EditorPanel } from './components/EditorPanel/EditorPanel';
import { BottomPanel } from './components/bottom/BottomPanel';
import { TerminalInputStandalone } from './components/TerminalInputStandalone';
import { eventBus } from '../../shared/services/eventBus';
import { useResizePanels } from '../../hooks/useResizePanels';
import { listProjects, getProjectProcesses, type Project, type Process } from './api/projectApi';
import { executeProcess, getExecutionStatus } from '../process-designer/api/processApi';
import { websocketLogs } from '../../shared/services/websocketLogs';
import '../process-designer/process-designer.css';


function SettingsPanel({ selectedProjectId }: { selectedProjectId: string | null }) {
  const { openFiles, activeFilePath } = useFileStore();
  const activeFile = activeFilePath ? openFiles.get(activeFilePath) : null;
  const [activeRightTab, setActiveRightTab] = useState('info');
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [selectedProject, setSelectedProject] = useState<string | null>(selectedProjectId);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [executingProcessId, setExecutingProcessId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Загрузка проектов
  useEffect(() => {
    loadProjects();
  }, []);

  // Загрузка процессов при выборе проекта
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedProject) {
      loadProcesses(selectedProject);
    }
  }, [selectedProject]);

  const loadProjects = async () => {
    const projs = await listProjects();
    setProjects(projs);
    if (!selectedProject && projs.length > 0) {
      setSelectedProject(projs[0].id);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async (projectId: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    const procs = await getProjectProcesses(projectId);
    setProcesses(procs);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleExecuteProcess = async (processId: string, projectPath: string) => {
    if (executingProcessId) {
      eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `⚠️ Already executing a process, please wait...` });
      return;
    }
    
    console.log('Execute clicked:', processId, projectPath);
    eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `🚀 Executing process ${processId}...` });
    setExecutingProcessId(processId);
    
    try {
      const result = await executeProcess(processId);
      const executionId = result.execution_id;
      
      eventBus.emit('process:started', { processId: executionId });
      eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `✅ Execution started: ${executionId}` });
      
      // Подключаемся к WebSocket для получения логов
      websocketLogs.connect(executionId, (logData) => {
        console.log('[WebSocket] Log received:', logData);
        eventBus.emit('process:log', {
          source: 'process',
          message: `[${logData.type}] ${JSON.stringify(logData.data)}`
        });
      });
      
      // Периодически проверяем статус
      const intervalId = setInterval(async () => {
        try {
          const status = await getExecutionStatus(executionId);
          console.log('Status:', status);
          
          // Обновляем статусы узлов
          if (status.node_statuses) {
            Object.entries(status.node_statuses).forEach(([nodeId, nodeStatus]: [string, any]) => {
              const nodeStatusStr = typeof nodeStatus === 'string' ? nodeStatus : nodeStatus.status;
              const nodeResult = typeof nodeStatus === 'object' ? nodeStatus.result : null;
              
              if (nodeStatusStr === 'completed' && nodeResult) {
                if (nodeResult.stdout) {
                  eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `✅ Node ${nodeId}: ${nodeResult.stdout}` });
                } else {
                  eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `✅ Node ${nodeId} completed` });
                }
              } else if (nodeStatusStr === 'failed') {
                const errorMsg = nodeResult?.error || 'Unknown error';
                eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `❌ Node ${nodeId} failed: ${errorMsg}` });
              }

## КОМАНДА: Создать TemplateList в едином стиле

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/templates/TemplateList.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import '../process-designer/process-designer.css';

interface Template {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  version: string;
  tags: string[];
  schedule: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export const TemplateList = () => {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<Template[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchTag, setSearchTag] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTemplates();
  }, [searchTag]);

  const loadTemplates = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const url = searchTag 
        ? `http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/list?tag=${searchTag}`
        : 'http://localhost:8010/api/v1/templates/list';
      const response = await fetch(url);
      const data = await response.json();
      setTemplates(data.templates || []);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Failed to load templates');
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (dateStr: string) => {
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    return date.toLocaleDateString();
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="process-designer-container" style={{ padding: 'var(--k-space-xl)' }}>
        <div style={{ color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)' }}>Loading templates...</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="process-designer-container">
      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ 
        padding: 'var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-md) var(--k-space-xl)',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)'
      }}>
        <h1 style={{ 
          fontSize: '20px', 
          fontWeight: 600, 
          color: 'var(--k-text-primary)', 
          margin: 0,
          marginBottom: 'var(--k-space-xs)'
        }}>
          Template Library
        </h1>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)', margin: 0 }}>
          Reusable process templates
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Toolbar */}
      <div style={{ 
        padding: 'var(--k-space-lg) var(--k-space-xl)',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'center'
      }}>
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Filter by tag..."
          value={searchTag}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchTag(e.target.value)}
          style={{
            padding: '6px 12px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-sm)',
            border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
            background: 'var(--k-bg-depth)',
            color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
            fontSize: '13px',
            width: '200px'
          }}
        />
        <button style={{
          padding: '6px 14px',
          borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-sm)',
          border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
          background: 'var(--k-bg-card)',
          color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
          fontSize: '12px',
          cursor: 'pointer',
          fontWeight: 500
        }}>
          + New Template
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Error */}
      {error && (
        <div style={{
          margin: 'var(--k-space-lg) var(--k-space-xl)',
          padding: 'var(--k-space-md)',
          background: 'var(--k-status-error-bg)',
          border: '1px solid var(--k-status-error)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-md)',
          color: 'var(--k-status-error)',
          fontSize: '13px'
        }}>
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Templates Grid */}
      {templates.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ 
          textAlign: 'center', 
          padding: '60px var(--k-space-xl)',
          color: 'var(--k-text-tertiary)'
        }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '48px', marginBottom: 'var(--k-space-md)' }}>📁</div>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: 'var(--k-space-xs)' }}>No templates</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '13px' }}>Create your first template or save an existing process as a template</div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ 
          padding: 'var(--k-space-xl)',
          display: 'grid',
          gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr))',
          gap: 'var(--k-space-lg)'
        }}>
          {templates.map((template) => (
            <div
              key={template.id}
              style={{
                background: 'var(--k-bg-card)',
                border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-lg)',
                transition: 'all var(--k-transition-fast)'
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'var(--k-accent-ocean)';
                e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--k-bg-hover)';
              }}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'var(--k-border)';
                e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--k-bg-card)';
              }}
            >
              {/* Card Header */}
              <div style={{
                padding: 'var(--k-space-lg)',
                borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'flex-start'
              }}>
                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--k-text-primary)', marginBottom: 'var(--k-space-xs)' }}>
                    {template.name}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--k-text-tertiary)' }}>
                    v{template.version}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 'var(--k-space-sm)' }}>
                  <button
                    title="Create process from template"
                    style={{
                      padding: '4px 8px',
                      borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-sm)',
                      border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                      background: 'var(--k-bg-depth)',
                      color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      cursor: 'pointer'
                    }}
                    onClick={() => alert('Instantiate: ' + template.id)}
                  >
                    Use
                  </button>
                  <button
                    title="Delete template"
                    style={{
                      padding: '4px 8px',
                      borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-sm)',
                      border: '1px solid var(--k-status-error)',
                      background: 'transparent',
                      color: 'var(--k-status-error)',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      cursor: 'pointer'
                    }}
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (confirm('Delete this template?')) {
                        handleDelete(template.id);
                      }
                    }}
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Card Body */}
              <div style={{ padding: 'var(--k-space-lg)' }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 'var(--k-space-md)' }}>
                  {template.description || 'No description'}
                </div>
                
                {/* Tags */}
                {template.tags && template.tags.length > 0 && (
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 'var(--k-space-sm)', marginBottom: 'var(--k-space-md)' }}>
                    {template.tags.map((tag) => (
                      <span
                        key={tag}
                        style={{
                          fontSize: '10px',
                          padding: '2px 6px',
                          borderRadius: 'var(--k-radius-sm)',
                          background: 'var(--k-bg-depth)',
                          color: 'var(--k-text-tertiary)',
                          border: '1px solid var(--k-border)'
                        }}
                      >
                        {tag}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Schedule */}
                {template.schedule && (
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--k-text-tertiary)' }}>
                    Schedule: {template.schedule}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Footer */}
                <div style={{ 
                  marginTop: 'var(--k-space-md)',
                  paddingTop: 'var(--k-space-md)',
                  borderTop: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                  fontSize: '10px',
                  color: 'var(--k-text-tertiary)'
                }}>
                  Updated: {formatDate(template.updated_at)}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
EOF
```

/* Global Design Tokens - Kymatics Design System */
:root {

/* Main Layout */

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/templates/TemplateList.html << 'EOF'
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>Templates Library - Kymatics Mockup</title>
  <style>
    * {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      box-sizing: border-box;
    }

    body {
      background: #0A0F1A;
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
      padding: 24px;
    }

    /* Kymatics Design Tokens */
    :root {
      --k-bg-abyss: #0A0F1A;
      --k-bg-depth: #111521;
      --k-bg-card: #1E2A3E;
      --k-bg-hover: #253349;
      --k-border: #2A3A50;
      --k-border-light: #3A4A60;
      --k-text-primary: #FFFFFF;
      --k-text-secondary: #8A9AB0;
      --k-text-tertiary: #5A6E8A;
      --k-accent-ocean: #4A9FFF;
      --k-accent-ocean-hover: #3A8FEF;
      --k-status-success: #2DD4BF;
      --k-status-error: #EF4444;
      --k-space-xs: 4px;
      --k-space-sm: 8px;
      --k-space-md: 12px;
      --k-space-lg: 16px;
      --k-space-xl: 24px;
      --k-radius-sm: 6px;
      --k-radius-md: 8px;
      --k-radius-lg: 12px;
    }

    /* Layout */
    .templates-container {
      max-width: 1400px;
      margin: 0 auto;
    }

    /* Header */
    .templates-header {
      padding: var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-md) var(--k-space-xl);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-xl);
    }

    .templates-title {
      font-size: 20px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-xs);
    }

    .templates-subtitle {
      font-size: 13px;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
    }

    /* Toolbar */
    .templates-toolbar {
      padding: 0 var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-xl);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      gap: var(--k-space-lg);
      flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .search-input {
      flex: 1;
      max-width: 300px;
      padding: 8px 12px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      font-size: 13px;
    }

    .search-input:focus {
      outline: none;
      border-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .btn-primary {
      padding: 8px 16px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: none;
      background: var(--k-accent-ocean);
      color: white;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 500;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background 0.2s;
    }

    .btn-primary:hover {
      background: var(--k-accent-ocean-hover);
    }

    .btn-secondary {
      padding: 6px 12px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      font-size: 12px;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: all 0.2s;
    }

    .btn-secondary:hover {
      background: var(--k-bg-hover);
      border-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .btn-danger {
      border-color: var(--k-status-error);
      color: var(--k-status-error);
    }

    .btn-danger:hover {
      background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);
    }

    /* Grid */
    .templates-grid {
      padding: 0 var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-xl) var(--k-space-xl);
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(380px, 1fr));
      gap: var(--k-space-lg);
    }

    /* Card */
    .template-card {
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-lg);
      transition: all 0.2s ease;
    }

    .template-card:hover {
      border-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
      background: var(--k-bg-hover);
      transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    .card-header {
      padding: var(--k-space-lg);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: flex-start;
    }

    .card-title-section {
      flex: 1;
    }

    .card-name {
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-xs);
      font-size: 16px;
    }

    .card-version {
      font-size: 11px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
    }

    .card-actions {
      display: flex;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
    }

    .card-body {
      padding: var(--k-space-lg);
    }

    .card-description {
      font-size: 13px;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      line-height: 1.5;
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    /* Tags */
    .tags-container {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    .tag {
      font-size: 10px;
      padding: 2px 8px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
    }

    /* Info rows */
    .info-row {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) 0;
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      font-size: 12px;
    }

    .info-label {
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
    }

    .info-value {
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      font-family: monospace;
      font-size: 11px;
    }

    .info-value.schedule {
      color: var(--k-status-success);
    }

    .card-footer {
      padding: var(--k-space-md) var(--k-space-lg);
      border-top: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
    }

    /* Detail Panel (Right Side) */
    .detail-panel {
      position: fixed;
      right: 0;
      top: 0;
      width: 400px;
      height: 100vh;
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      border-left: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      transform: translateX(100%);
      transition: transform 0.3s ease;
      z-index: 1000;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }

    .detail-panel.open {
      transform: translateX(0);
    }

    .detail-header {
      padding: var(--k-space-lg);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
    }

    .detail-title {
      font-size: 18px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
    }

    .close-btn {
      background: none;
      border: none;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      font-size: 24px;
      cursor: pointer;
    }

    .close-btn:hover {
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
    }

    .detail-body {
      flex: 1;
      overflow-y: auto;
      padding: var(--k-space-lg);
    }

    .detail-section {
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-xl);
    }

    .detail-section-title {
      font-size: 12px;
      font-weight: 600;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    .yaml-preview {
      background: var(--k-bg-abyss);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-md);
      padding: var(--k-space-md);
      font-family: 'Monaco', monospace;
      font-size: 11px;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      overflow-x: auto;
      white-space: pre-wrap;
      max-height: 300px;
    }

    .overlay {
      position: fixed;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      bottom: 0;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
      z-index: 999;
      display: none;
    }

    .overlay.open {
      display: block;
    }

    /* Empty state */
    .empty-state {
      text-align: center;
      padding: 80px var(--k-space-xl);
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
    }

    .empty-icon {
      font-size: 64px;
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-lg);
      opacity: 0.5;
    }

    .empty-title {
      font-size: 18px;
      font-weight: 500;
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-sm);
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
    }

    .empty-text {
      font-size: 13px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="templates-container">
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="templates-header">
      <h1 class="templates-title">Template Library</h1>
      <p class="templates-subtitle">Reusable process templates for automation workflows</p>
    </div>

    <!-- Toolbar -->
    <div class="templates-toolbar">
      <input type="text" class="search-input" placeholder="Search by tag or name..." id="searchInput">
      <button class="btn-primary" id="newTemplateBtn">+ New Template</button>
    </div>

    <!-- Templates Grid -->
    <div class="templates-grid" id="templatesGrid">
      <!-- Card 1 -->
      <div class="template-card" data-template-id="1">
        <div class="card-header">
          <div class="card-title-section">
            <div class="card-name">Telegram News Collector</div>
            <div class="card-version">v1.0.0</div>
          </div>
          <div class="card-actions">
            <button class="btn-secondary view-details">Details</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary use-template">Use</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary btn-danger delete-template">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
          <div class="card-description">
            Collects messages from Telegram channels and saves them to a local file. Supports multiple channels and keyword filtering.
          </div>
          <div class="tags-container">
            <span class="tag">telegram</span>
            <span class="tag">collector</span>
            <span class="tag">messaging</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Inputs</span>
            <span class="info-value">bot_token, channels, keywords</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Outputs</span>
            <span class="info-value">messages, count</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Schedule</span>
            <span class="info-value schedule">0 */6 * * * (every 6 hours)</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-footer">
          <span>Updated: 2026-05-12</span>
          <span>Steps: 4</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Card 2 -->
      <div class="template-card" data-template-id="2">
        <div class="card-header">
          <div class="card-title-section">
            <div class="card-name">Database Backup</div>
            <div class="card-version">v2.1.0</div>
          </div>
          <div class="card-actions">
            <button class="btn-secondary view-details">Details</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary use-template">Use</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary btn-danger delete-template">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
          <div class="card-description">
            Automated database backup with compression and upload to S3. Supports PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQLite.
          </div>
          <div class="tags-container">
            <span class="tag">database</span>
            <span class="tag">backup</span>
            <span class="tag">s3</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Inputs</span>
            <span class="info-value">db_type, host, port, user, password, database</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Outputs</span>
            <span class="info-value">backup_file, s3_url, size</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Schedule</span>
            <span class="info-value schedule">0 2 * * * (daily at 2 AM)</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-footer">
          <span>Updated: 2026-05-10</span>
          <span>Steps: 6</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Card 3 -->
      <div class="template-card" data-template-id="3">
        <div class="card-header">
          <div class="card-title-section">
            <div class="card-name">API Health Monitor</div>
            <div class="card-version">v1.2.0</div>
          </div>
          <div class="card-actions">
            <button class="btn-secondary view-details">Details</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary use-template">Use</button>
            <button class="btn-secondary btn-danger delete-template">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
          <div class="card-description">
            Monitors API endpoints health, checks response time and status codes. Sends alerts on failures.
          </div>
          <div class="tags-container">
            <span class="tag">monitoring</span>
            <span class="tag">api</span>
            <span class="tag">alerts</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Inputs</span>
            <span class="info-value">endpoints, interval, alert_webhook</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Outputs</span>
            <span class="info-value">status, response_time, uptime_percentage</span>
          </div>
          <div class="info-row">
            <span class="info-label">Schedule</span>
            <span class="info-value schedule">*/5 * * * * (every 5 minutes)</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-footer">
          <span>Updated: 2026-05-08</span>
          <span>Steps: 5</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Detail Panel -->
  <div class="overlay" id="overlay"></div>
  <div class="detail-panel" id="detailPanel">
    <div class="detail-header">
      <h2 class="detail-title" id="detailTitle">Template Details</h2>
      <button class="close-btn" id="closePanelBtn">&times;</button>
    </div>
    <div class="detail-body" id="detailBody">
      <div class="detail-section">
        <div class="detail-section-title">Description</div>
        <div id="detailDescription" style="color: var(--k-text-secondary); font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.5;"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="detail-section">
        <div class="detail-section-title">Input Parameters</div>
        <div id="detailInputs" style="color: var(--k-text-secondary); font-size: 12px;"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="detail-section">
        <div class="detail-section-title">Output Parameters</div>
        <div id="detailOutputs" style="color: var(--k-text-secondary); font-size: 12px;"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="detail-section">
        <div class="detail-section-title">Steps / Workflow</div>
        <div id="detailSteps" style="color: var(--k-text-secondary); font-size: 12px;"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="detail-section">
        <div class="detail-section-title">YAML Definition</div>
        <div class="yaml-preview" id="detailYaml"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="detail-section">
        <div class="detail-section-title">Actions</div>
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; margin-top: 12px;">
          <button class="btn-primary" id="editTemplateBtn">Edit Template</button>
          <button class="btn-secondary" id="duplicateTemplateBtn">Duplicate</button>
          <button class="btn-secondary btn-danger" id="deleteFromPanelBtn">Delete</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // Mock data for templates
    const templatesData = {
      1: {
        name: 'Telegram News Collector',
        description: 'Collects messages from Telegram channels and saves them to a local file. Supports multiple channels and keyword filtering.',
        inputs: ['bot_token (string, required)', 'channels (list, required)', 'keywords (list, optional)'],
        outputs: ['messages (string)', 'count (integer)'],
        steps: [
          '1. Fetch updates from Telegram API',
          '2. Filter messages by keywords',
          '3. Format messages as JSON',
          '4. Save to file'
        ],
        yaml: `template:
  id: telegram_collector
  name: Telegram News Collector
  inputs:
    - bot_token: string
    - channels: list
    - keywords: list
  steps:
    - http_get: https://api.telegram.org/bot\${bot_token}/getUpdates
    - filter: messages contains keywords
    - json_parse: extract messages
    - file_write: ./telegram_messages.json`
      },
      2: {
        name: 'Database Backup',
        description: 'Automated database backup with compression and upload to S3.',
        inputs: ['db_type (string)', 'host (string)', 'port (integer)', 'user (string)', 'password (secret)', 'database (string)'],
        outputs: ['backup_file (string)', 's3_url (string)', 'size (integer)'],
        steps: [
          '1. Connect to database',
          '2. Run pg_dump/mysqldump',
          '3. Compress backup',
          '4. Upload to S3',
          '5. Clean old backups'
        ],
        yaml: `template:
  id: db_backup
  name: Database Backup
  steps:
    - bash: pg_dump -h \${host} -U \${user} \${database} > backup.sql
    - bash: gzip backup.sql
    - s3_upload: backup.sql.gz`
      },
      3: {
        name: 'API Health Monitor',
        description: 'Monitors API endpoints health and sends alerts.',
        inputs: ['endpoints (list)', 'interval (integer)', 'alert_webhook (string)'],
        outputs: ['status (string)', 'response_time (integer)', 'uptime_percentage (float)'],
        steps: [
          '1. Loop through endpoints',
          '2. HTTP GET request',
          '3. Check response code',
          '4. Measure response time',
          '5. Send alert on failure',
          '6. Update dashboard'
        ],
        yaml: `template:
  id: api_monitor
  name: API Health Monitor
  steps:
    - for_each: endpoints
    - http_get: \${endpoint.url}
    - condition: status == 200
    - http_post: \${alert_webhook} on failure`
      }
    };

    // Open detail panel
    function showDetails(templateId) {
      const data = templatesData[templateId];
      if (!data) return;

      document.getElementById('detailTitle').textContent = data.name;
      document.getElementById('detailDescription').textContent = data.description;
      document.getElementById('detailInputs').innerHTML = data.inputs.map(i => `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">• ${i}</div>`).join('');
      document.getElementById('detailOutputs').innerHTML = data.outputs.map(o => `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">• ${o}</div>`).join('');
      document.getElementById('detailSteps').innerHTML = data.steps.map(s => `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">${s}</div>`).join('');
      document.getElementById('detailYaml').textContent = data.yaml;

      document.getElementById('overlay').classList.add('open');
      document.getElementById('detailPanel').classList.add('open');
    }

    function closeDetails() {
      document.getElementById('overlay').classList.remove('open');
      document.getElementById('detailPanel').classList.remove('open');
    }

    // Event listeners
    document.querySelectorAll('.view-details').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const card = btn.closest('.template-card');
        const id = card.dataset.templateId;
        showDetails(id);
      });
    });

    document.getElementById('closePanelBtn').addEventListener('click', closeDetails);
    document.getElementById('overlay').addEventListener('click', closeDetails);

    document.getElementById('editTemplateBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Edit template functionality - will open YAML editor');
    });

    document.getElementById('duplicateTemplateBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Duplicate template - will create a copy');
    });

    document.getElementById('newTemplateBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Create new template - will open template editor');
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.use-template').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        alert('Instantiate template - will create a new process');
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.delete-template').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this template?')) {
          const card = btn.closest('.template-card');
          card.remove();
        }
      });
    });

    // Search functionality
    document.getElementById('searchInput').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      const searchTerm = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
      const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.template-card');
      cards.forEach(card => {
        const name = card.querySelector('.card-name').textContent.toLowerCase();
        const description = card.querySelector('.card-description').textContent.toLowerCase();
        const tags = Array.from(card.querySelectorAll('.tag')).map(t => t.textContent.toLowerCase());
        const matches = name.includes(searchTerm) || description.includes(searchTerm) || tags.some(t => t.includes(searchTerm));
        card.style.display = matches ? 'block' : 'none';
      });
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

### **Концепция: 3-колоночный макет** (аналогично Process Designer)

## 🎨 **Режимы просмотра**

### **1. Режим "Библиотека" (просмотр всех элементов)**
- В центральной колонке список элементов с краткой информацией
- Клик по элементу открывает его в форме редактирования

### **2. Режим "Редактор" (форма + превью)**
- Текущий макет (3 колонки)
- Живой YAML превью справа

### **3. Режим "Сравнение" (для будущего)**
- Выбор 2-3 элементов, сравнение параметров

## 🏷️ **Типы элементов в Library**

| Тип | Описание | Пример |
|-----|----------|--------|
| **Process** | Полноценные процессы BPMN | `telegram_collector` |
| **Agent** | Специализированные агенты | `coder_agent`, `architect_agent` |
| **Skill** | Атомарные действия | `http_get`, `bash_exec` |
| **Template** | Шаблоны процессов | `db_backup_template` |
| **Integration** (future) | Внешние API связи | `slack_connector`, `google_drive` |

## 📝 **Спецификация для каждого типа компонента**

### **Process**
- Steps workflow (BPMN-like)
- Может содержать condition, parallel, for_each

### **Agent**
- agent_name, task, model, temperature, system_prompt
- Может быть вызван из Process

### **Skill**
- executor (http_node, bash, file_node, agent, python)
- Минимальная единица, не требует workflow

### **Template**
- Содержит YAML целиком
- Используется для генерации процессов

### **Integration**
- external_service, api_key_required, endpoint
- Для будущих подключений (Slack, Google, AWS)

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Хорошо! Сделаем гибкую систему с разными структурами для каждого типа.

## 📁 **Структуры компонентов (предварительные)**

### **Process**
```yaml
type: process
name: telegram_collector
description: Collects messages from Telegram channels
version: 1.0.0
tags: [telegram, collector]
schedule: "0 */6 * * *"
steps:
  - id: fetch
    type: http_node
    config:
      url: https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/getUpdates
  - id: parse
    type: agent
    config:
      task: Extract messages from JSON
      agent_name: coder
  - id: save
    type: file_node
    config:
      operation: write
      path: ./messages.json
inputs:
  - name: bot_token
    type: string
    required: true
  - name: channels
    type: list
    required: true
outputs:
  - name: messages
    type: string
  - name: count
    type: integer
```

### **Agent**
```yaml
type: agent
name: code_reviewer
description: Reviews code for best practices
version: 1.0.0
tags: [code, review]
config:
  model: qwen2.5-coder:7b
  temperature: 0.3
  max_tokens: 4000
  system_prompt: "You are a senior code reviewer..."
  task_template: "Review this code: ${code}"
timeout: 60
```

### **Skill**
```yaml
type: skill
name: http_get
description: Executes HTTP GET request
version: 1.0.0
tags: [http, api]
executor: http_node
inputs:
  - name: url
    type: string
    required: true
  - name: headers
    type: dict
    required: false
    default: {}
  - name: timeout
    type: integer
    required: false
    default: 30
outputs:
  - name: body
    type: string
  - name: status
    type: integer
  - name: headers
    type: dict
config_template:
  method: GET
  url: ${url}
  headers: ${headers}
  timeout: ${timeout}
```

### **Template**
```yaml
type: template
name: db_backup
description: Database backup template
version: 1.0.0
tags: [database, backup]
schedule: "0 2 * * *"
yaml: |
  template:
    id: db_backup
    name: Database Backup
    steps:
      - bash: pg_dump > backup.sql
      - s3_upload: backup.sql
inputs:
  - name: db_host
    type: string
  - name: db_user
    type: string
outputs:
  - name: backup_url
    type: string
```

### **Integration**
```yaml
type: integration
name: slack_connector
description: Slack API integration
version: 0.1.0
tags: [slack, notification]
external_service: slack
auth_type: oauth2
config:
  client_id: ""
  client_secret: ""
  scopes: ["chat:write", "channels:read"]
endpoints:
  - name: send_message
    url: https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage
    method: POST
  - name: list_channels
    url: https://slack.com/api/conversations.list
    method: GET
status: draft  # draft, published, deprecated
```
