```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/components/ComponentsPage.html << 'EOF'
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>Kymatics Components - Mockup</title>
  <style>
    * {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      box-sizing: border-box;
    }

    body {
      background: #0A0F1A;
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
      height: 100vh;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    /* Kymatics Design Tokens */
    :root {
      --k-bg-abyss: #0A0F1A;
      --k-bg-depth: #111521;
      --k-bg-card: #1E2A3E;
      --k-bg-hover: #253349;
      --k-border: #2A3A50;
      --k-border-light: #3A4A60;
      --k-text-primary: #FFFFFF;
      --k-text-secondary: #8A9AB0;
      --k-text-tertiary: #5A6E8A;
      --k-accent-ocean: #4A9FFF;
      --k-accent-ocean-hover: #3A8FEF;
      --k-status-success: #2DD4BF;
      --k-status-error: #EF4444;
      --k-status-warning: #F59E0B;
      --k-space-xs: 4px;
      --k-space-sm: 8px;
      --k-space-md: 12px;
      --k-space-lg: 16px;
      --k-space-xl: 24px;
      --k-radius-sm: 6px;
      --k-radius-md: 8px;
      --k-radius-lg: 12px;
    }

    /* Layout */
    .components-app {
      height: 100vh;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }

    /* Header */
    .components-header {
      padding: var(--k-space-lg) var(--k-space-xl);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
    }

    .components-title {
      font-size: 20px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
    }

    .components-subtitle {
      font-size: 12px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      margin-top: var(--k-space-xs);
    }

    .btn-primary {
      padding: 8px 16px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: none;
      background: var(--k-accent-ocean);
      color: white;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 500;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background 0.2s;
    }

    .btn-primary:hover {
      background: var(--k-accent-ocean-hover);
    }

    /* 3-Column Layout */
    .components-main {
      flex: 1;
      display: flex;
      min-height: 0;
    }

    /* Column 1: Navigation (260px) */
    .col-nav {
      width: 260px;
      background: var(--k-bg-abyss);
      border-right: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

    .nav-section {
      padding: var(--k-space-md) var(--k-space-lg);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
    }

    .nav-section-title {
      font-size: 10px;
      font-weight: 600;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    .type-list {
      list-style: none;
    }

    .type-item {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) var(--k-space-md);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      cursor: pointer;
      margin-bottom: 2px;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      font-size: 13px;
    }

    .type-item:hover {
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
    }

    .type-item.active {
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
      color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .type-badge {
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      padding: 2px 6px;
      border-radius: 10px;
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
    }

    .search-input {
      width: 100%;
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) var(--k-space-md);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      font-size: 12px;
    }

    .search-input:focus {
      outline: none;
      border-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .tags-list {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
    }

    .tag-filter {
      font-size: 11px;
      padding: 3px 8px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      cursor: pointer;
      border: 1px solid transparent;
    }

    .tag-filter:hover {
      border-color: var(--k-border);
    }

    .tag-filter.active {
      background: var(--k-accent-ocean);
      color: white;
    }

    .draft-item {
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) var(--k-space-md);
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      border-left: 2px solid var(--k-status-warning);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-sm);
      font-size: 12px;
    }

    /* Column 2: Editor (1fr) */
    .col-editor {
      flex: 1;
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

    .editor-header {
      padding: var(--k-space-lg) var(--k-space-xl);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
    }

    .editor-tabs {
      display: flex;
      gap: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    .editor-tab {
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) var(--k-space-md);
      font-size: 12px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      cursor: pointer;
      border-bottom: 2px solid transparent;
    }

    .editor-tab.active {
      color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
      border-bottom-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .editor-form {
      padding: var(--k-space-xl);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: var(--k-space-lg);
    }

    .form-field {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: var(--k-space-xs);
    }

    .form-label {
      font-size: 12px;
      font-weight: 500;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
    }

    .form-input, .form-textarea, .form-select {
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) var(--k-space-md);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      font-size: 13px;
      font-family: inherit;
    }

    .form-textarea {
      resize: vertical;
      min-height: 80px;
    }

    .form-input:focus, .form-textarea:focus {
      outline: none;
      border-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .input-group {
      display: flex;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
      align-items: center;
    }

    .input-group .form-input {
      flex: 1;
    }

    .array-item {
      display: flex;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-sm);
      align-items: center;
    }

    .array-item .form-input {
      flex: 1;
    }

    .btn-icon {
      padding: 6px 10px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      cursor: pointer;
      font-size: 12px;
    }

    .btn-icon:hover {
      background: var(--k-bg-hover);
    }

    .btn-danger {
      border-color: var(--k-status-error);
      color: var(--k-status-error);
    }

    .section {
      border-top: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      padding-top: var(--k-space-lg);
      margin-top: var(--k-space-sm);
    }

    .section-title {
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    /* Column 3: Preview (360px) */
    .col-preview {
      width: 360px;
      background: var(--k-bg-abyss);
      border-left: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

    .preview-section {
      padding: var(--k-space-lg);
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
    }

    .preview-title {
      font-size: 11px;
      font-weight: 600;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      margin-bottom: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    .yaml-preview {
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-md);
      padding: var(--k-space-md);
      font-family: 'Monaco', monospace;
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      overflow-x: auto;
      white-space: pre-wrap;
      max-height: 200px;
    }

    .stats-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
      gap: var(--k-space-md);
    }

    .stat-item {
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      padding: var(--k-space-md);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-md);
    }

    .stat-label {
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
    }

    .stat-value {
      font-size: 18px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
    }

    .tags-container {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
    }

    .tag {
      font-size: 10px;
      padding: 2px 8px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
    }

    .action-buttons {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: var(--k-space-sm);
    }

    .action-btn {
      flex: 1;
      padding: var(--k-space-sm) var(--k-space-md);
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
      color: var(--k-text-primary);
      font-size: 12px;
      cursor: pointer;
      text-align: center;
    }

    .action-btn.primary {
      background: var(--k-accent-ocean);
      border-color: var(--k-accent-ocean);
    }

    .action-btn.danger {
      border-color: var(--k-status-error);
      color: var(--k-status-error);
    }

    .auto-save-indicator {
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--k-status-success);
      margin-left: var(--k-space-md);
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="components-app">
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="components-header">
      <div>
        <div class="components-title">Components Library</div>
        <div class="components-subtitle">Manage processes, agents, skills, templates, and integrations</div>
      </div>
      <button class="btn-primary" id="newComponentBtn">+ New Component</button>
    </div>

    <!-- Main 3-Column Layout -->
    <div class="components-main">
      <!-- Column 1: Navigation -->
      <div class="col-nav">
        <div class="nav-section">
          <div class="nav-section-title">Types</div>
          <ul class="type-list">
            <li class="type-item active" data-type="process">
              <span>📄 Processes</span>
              <span class="type-badge">8</span>
            </li>
            <li class="type-item" data-type="agent">
              <span>🤖 Agents</span>
              <span class="type-badge">3</span>
            </li>
            <li class="type-item" data-type="skill">
              <span>⚡ Skills</span>
              <span class="type-badge">10</span>
            </li>
            <li class="type-item" data-type="template">
              <span>📦 Templates</span>
              <span class="type-badge">5</span>
            </li>
            <li class="type-item" data-type="integration">
              <span>🔌 Integrations</span>
              <span class="type-badge">0</span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>

        <div class="nav-section">
          <div class="nav-section-title">Search</div>
          <input type="text" class="search-input" placeholder="Search by name..." id="searchInput">
        </div>

        <div class="nav-section">
          <div class="nav-section-title">Tags</div>
          <div class="tags-list" id="tagsFilter">
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="telegram">telegram (3)</span>
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="database">database (2)</span>
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="api">api (5)</span>
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="backup">backup (1)</span>
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="monitoring">monitoring (3)</span>
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="http">http (4)</span>
            <span class="tag-filter" data-tag="code">code (2)</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="nav-section">
          <div class="nav-section-title">Drafts</div>
          <div id="draftsList">
            <div class="draft-item">telegram_collector_edit (Process)</div>
            <div class="draft-item">new_api_skill (Skill)</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Column 2: Editor -->
      <div class="col-editor">
        <div class="editor-header">
          <div class="editor-tabs">
            <div class="editor-tab active" data-mode="form">Form Editor</div>
            <div class="editor-tab" data-mode="yaml">YAML Editor</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span class="auto-save-indicator" id="autoSaveIndicator">✓ Auto-saved 2s ago</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="editor-form" id="editorForm">
          <!-- Process Form (active by default) -->
          <div class="form-field">
            <label class="form-label">Name *</label>
            <input type="text" class="form-input" id="nameInput" value="telegram_news_collector">
          </div>

          <div class="form-field">
            <label class="form-label">Description</label>
            <textarea class="form-textarea" id="descInput">Collects messages from Telegram channels and saves them to a local file</textarea>
          </div>

          <div class="input-group">
            <div class="form-field" style="flex: 1;">
              <label class="form-label">Version</label>
              <input type="text" class="form-input" id="versionInput" value="1.0.0">
            </div>
            <div class="form-field" style="flex: 2;">
              <label class="form-label">Tags</label>
              <div class="input-group">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Add tag..." id="tagInput">
                <button class="btn-icon" id="addTagBtn">+ Add</button>
              </div>
              <div class="tags-container" id="tagsContainer" style="margin-top: 8px;">
                <span class="tag">telegram</span>
                <span class="tag">collector</span>
                <span class="tag">messaging</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Inputs Section -->
          <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">Inputs</div>
            <div id="inputsList">
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="bot_token">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 100px;">
                  <option>string</option>
                  <option>integer</option>
                  <option>boolean</option>
                  <option>list</option>
                  <option>secret</option>
                </select>
                <input type="checkbox" id="required1" checked> <label style="font-size: 12px;">required</label>
                <button class="btn-icon btn-danger">🗑</button>
              </div>
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="channels">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 100px;">
                  <option>string</option>
                  <option>integer</option>
                  <option>boolean</option>
                  <option selected>list</option>
                  <option>secret</option>
                </select>
                <input type="checkbox" id="required2" checked> <label style="font-size: 12px;">required</label>
                <button class="btn-icon btn-danger">🗑</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button class="btn-icon" id="addInputBtn" style="margin-top: 8px;">+ Add Input</button>
          </div>

          <!-- Outputs Section -->
          <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">Outputs</div>
            <div id="outputsList">
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="messages">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 100px;">
                  <option selected>string</option>
                  <option>integer</option>
                  <option>boolean</option>
                  <option>list</option>
                  <option>object</option>
                </select>
                <button class="btn-icon btn-danger">🗑</button>
              </div>
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="count">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 100px;">
                  <option>string</option>
                  <option selected>integer</option>
                  <option>boolean</option>
                  <option>list</option>
                  <option>object</option>
                </select>
                <button class="btn-icon btn-danger">🗑</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button class="btn-icon" id="addOutputBtn" style="margin-top: 8px;">+ Add Output</button>
          </div>

          <!-- Steps Section (Process only) -->
          <div class="section" id="stepsSection">
            <div class="section-title">Workflow Steps</div>
            <div id="stepsList">
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Step ID" value="fetch_messages" style="width: 150px;">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 120px;">
                  <option selected>http_node</option>
                  <option>agent</option>
                  <option>bash</option>
                  <option>file_node</option>
                  <option>condition</option>
                </select>
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Config" value='{"url": "https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/getUpdates"}'>
                <button class="btn-icon btn-danger">🗑</button>
              </div>
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Step ID" value="parse_json" style="width: 150px;">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 120px;">
                  <option>http_node</option>
                  <option selected>agent</option>
                  <option>bash</option>
                  <option>file_node</option>
                  <option>condition</option>
                </select>
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Config" value='{"task": "Extract messages from JSON"}'>
                <button class="btn-icon btn-danger">🗑</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button class="btn-icon" id="addStepBtn" style="margin-top: 8px;">+ Add Step</button>
          </div>

          <!-- Schedule -->
          <div class="form-field">
            <label class="form-label">Schedule (cron)</label>
            <input type="text" class="form-input" id="scheduleInput" value="0 */6 * * *">
          </div>

          <!-- Form Actions -->
          <div class="input-group" style="margin-top: var(--k-space-lg); padding-top: var(--k-space-lg); border-top: 1px solid var(--k-border);">
            <button class="action-btn primary" id="saveBtn">💾 Save</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="resetBtn">🔄 Reset</button>
            <button class="action-btn danger" id="deleteBtn">🗑 Delete</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="instantiateBtn">🚀 Instantiate</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Column 3: Preview/Info -->
      <div class="col-preview">
        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">YAML Preview</div>
          <div class="yaml-preview" id="yamlPreview">
type: process
name: telegram_news_collector
version: 1.0.0
tags: [telegram, collector, messaging]
inputs:
  - bot_token: string (required)
  - channels: list (required)
outputs:
  - messages: string
  - count: integer
schedule: "0 */6 * * *"
steps:
  - fetch_messages: http_node
  - parse_json: agent
  - save_to_file: file_node
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">Statistics</div>
          <div class="stats-grid">
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Created</div>
              <div class="stat-value">2026-05-12</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Updated</div>
              <div class="stat-value">2h ago</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Used</div>
              <div class="stat-value">15x</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Rating</div>
              <div class="stat-value">4.8/5</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">Dependencies</div>
          <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--k-text-secondary);">
            <div>• Requires: http_node, agent, file_node</div>
            <div>• Used by: project_alpha, monitoring_dashboard</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">Actions</div>
          <div class="action-buttons">
            <button class="action-btn">📋 Duplicate</button>
            <button class="action-btn">📤 Export YAML</button>
            <button class="action-btn">📊 Usage History</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // Auto-save simulation
    let lastSave = Date.now();
    const autoSaveIndicator = document.getElementById('autoSaveIndicator');
    
    setInterval(() => {
      lastSave = Date.now();
      autoSaveIndicator.textContent = '✓ Auto-saved just now';
      autoSaveIndicator.style.color = 'var(--k-status-success)';
      setTimeout(() => {
        autoSaveIndicator.textContent = '✓ Auto-saved ' + Math.floor((Date.now() - lastSave) / 1000) + 's ago';
      }, 2000);
    }, 30000);

    // Tab switching (Form/YAML)
    document.querySelectorAll('.editor-tab').forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.editor-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        tab.classList.add('active');
        // TODO: Switch between form and YAML editor
        alert('YAML Editor mode - would show monaco editor here');
      });
    });

    // Type switching
    document.querySelectorAll('.type-item').forEach(item => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.type-item').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        item.classList.add('active');
        const type = item.dataset.type;
        
        // Show/hide type-specific sections
        const stepsSection = document.getElementById('stepsSection');
        if (type === 'process') {
          stepsSection.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
          stepsSection.style.display = 'none';
        }
        
        alert(`Switched to ${type} editor - would load appropriate form`);
      });
    });

    // Add input row
    document.getElementById('addInputBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const container = document.getElementById('inputsList');
      const newRow = document.createElement('div');
      newRow.className = 'array-item';
      newRow.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name">
        <select class="form-select" style="width: 100px;">
          <option>string</option>
          <option>integer</option>
          <option>boolean</option>
          <option>list</option>
          <option>secret</option>
        </select>
        <input type="checkbox"> <label style="font-size: 12px;">required</label>
        <button class="btn-icon btn-danger" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">🗑</button>
      `;
      container.appendChild(newRow);
    });

    // Add output row
    document.getElementById('addOutputBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const container = document.getElementById('outputsList');
      const newRow = document.createElement('div');
      newRow.className = 'array-item';
      newRow.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name">
        <select class="form-select" style="width: 100px;">
          <option>string</option>
          <option>integer</option>
          <option>boolean</option>
          <option>list</option>
          <option>object</option>
        </select>
        <button class="btn-icon btn-danger" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">🗑</button>
      `;
      container.appendChild(newRow);
    });

    // Add step row
    document.getElementById('addStepBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const container = document.getElementById('stepsList');
      const newRow = document.createElement('div');
      newRow.className = 'array-item';
      newRow.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Step ID" style="width: 150px;">
        <select class="form-select" style="width: 120px;">
          <option>http_node</option>
          <option>agent</option>
          <option>bash</option>
          <option>file_node</option>
          <option>condition</option>
        </select>
        <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Config" style="flex: 1;">
        <button class="btn-icon btn-danger" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">🗑</button>
      `;
      container.appendChild(newRow);
    });

    // Save button
    document.getElementById('saveBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Component saved to localStorage (draft mode)');
    });

    // New component
    document.getElementById('newComponentBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Create new component - would open fresh form');
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

1. ✅ Нравится ли 3-колоночная структура?
2. ✅ Какие элементы нужно добавить/убрать?
3. ✅ Нужно ли менять расположение блоков?
4. ✅ Какие кнопки должны быть в разных типах компонентов?
5. ✅ Для Skills - нужна ли визуальная форма или только YAML?
6. ✅ Для Integration - какие поля необходимы?

После утверждения перенесем в React с полной функциональностью (localStorage drafts, автосохранение, API синхронизация).

## 🔧 **Поля для Integration**

```yaml
integration:
  # Обязательные
  name: slack_connector
  description: Slack API integration
  version: 1.0.0
  
  # Конфигурация подключения
  auth_type: oauth2 | api_key | basic | none
  auth_config:
    client_id: ""
    client_secret: ""
    api_key_header: "Authorization"
    token_url: "https://slack.com/api/oauth.v2.access"
  
  # Endpoints
  endpoints:
    - name: send_message
      path: /chat.postMessage
      method: POST
      request_schema:
        channel: string (required)
        text: string (required)
      response_schema:
        ok: boolean
        message_ts: string
      rate_limit: 1/sec
  
  # Глобальные настройки
  base_url: "https://slack.com/api"
  timeout: 30
  retry_policy: 3
```

```bash
    :root {

      --k-status-warning: #F59E0B;

      --k-space-sm: 6px;
      --k-space-md: 10px;
      --k-space-lg: 14px;
      --k-space-xl: 18px;
      --k-radius-sm: 4px;
      --k-radius-md: 6px;
      --k-radius-lg: 8px;
    }

    /* Layout */
    .components-app {
      height: 100vh;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }

    /* Header */

    .btn-primary {
      padding: 6px 14px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: none;
      background: var(--k-accent);
      color: white;
      font-size: 12px;
      font-weight: 500;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background 0.2s;
    }

    .btn-primary:hover {
      background: var(--k-accent-hover);
    }

    /* 3-Column Layout */
    .components-main {
      flex: 1;

      min-height: 0;
    }

    /* Column 1: Navigation (240px) */
    .col-nav {
      width: 240px;
      background: var(--k-bg-abyss);

      flex-direction: column;

    }

    .nav-section {
      padding: 10px 12px;
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--k-border);
    }

    .nav-section:last-child {
      border-bottom: none;
    }

    .nav-section-title {
      font-size: 10px;
      font-weight: 500;

      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
      margin-bottom: 8px;
    }

    .search-input {
      width: 100%;
      padding: 6px 10px;
      border-radius: var(--k-radius-sm);
      border: 1px solid var(--k-border);

      font-size: 12px;

    .search-input:focus {
      outline: none;

    }

    .tags-row {
      display: flex;

    .tag-filter {
      font-size: 10px;

    }

    .tag-filter:hover {
      background: var(--k-bg-card);
    }

    .type-list {
      list-style: none;
    }

    .type-item {

    }

    .type-item:hover {
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);
    }

    .type-item.active {
      background: var(--k-bg-card);

    }

    .type-badge {
      background: var(--k-bg-depth);

      border-radius: 10px;

      color: var(--k-text-tertiary);
    }

    .component-list {
      max-height: 200px;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }

      padding: 5px 8px;

    /* Column 2: Editor (1fr) */
    .col-editor {

    }

    .dep-text {
      font-size: 11px;
      color: var(--k-text-secondary);
      line-height: 1.4;
    }
  </style>
</head>

        </div>

          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-field">
              <label class="form-label">Name</label>
              <input type="text" class="form-input" id="nameInput" value="telegram_collector">
            </div>

              <label class="form-label">Version</label>
              <input type="text" class="form-input" id="versionInput" value="1.0.0" style="width: 100px;">
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="form-field">
            <label class="form-label">Description</label>
            <textarea class="form-textarea" id="descInput">Collects messages from Telegram channels and saves to file</textarea>
          </div>

            <label class="form-label">Tags</label>

              <span class="tag">telegram</span>
              <span class="tag">collector</span>
              <span class="tag">messaging</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">Inputs</div>
            <div id="inputsList">
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="bot_token">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 90px;">
                  <option>string</option>
                  <option>integer</option>
                  <option>boolean</option>
                  <option selected>string</option>
                  <option>secret</option>
                </select>
                <label style="font-size: 11px;"><input type="checkbox" checked> req</label>
                <button class="btn-icon" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
              </div>
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="channels">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 90px;">
                  <option>string</option>
                  <option selected>list</option>
                  <option>integer</option>
                </select>
                <label style="font-size: 11px;"><input type="checkbox" checked> req</label>
                <button class="btn-icon" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button class="btn-icon" id="addInputBtn" style="margin-top: 6px;">+ Add Input</button>
          </div>

          <!-- Outputs -->
          <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">Outputs</div>
            <div id="outputsList">
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="messages">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 90px;">
                  <option selected>string</option>
                  <option>integer</option>
                  <option>list</option>
                </select>
                <button class="btn-icon" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
              </div>
              <div class="array-item">
                <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name" value="count">
                <select class="form-select" style="width: 90px;">
                  <option>string</option>
                  <option selected>integer</option>
                </select>
                <button class="btn-icon" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button class="btn-icon" id="addOutputBtn" style="margin-top: 6px;">+ Add Output</button>
          </div>

          <!-- Schedule -->
          <div class="form-field">
            <label class="form-label">Schedule (cron)</label>
            <input type="text" class="form-input" id="scheduleInput" value="0 */6 * * *">
          </div>

          <!-- Actions -->
          <div class="action-bar">
            <button class="action-btn primary" id="saveBtn">Save</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="duplicateBtn">Duplicate</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="exportBtn">Export</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="deleteBtn">Delete</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="instantiateBtn">Instantiate</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Column 3: Preview -->
      <div class="col-preview">
        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">YAML Preview</div>
          <div class="yaml-preview" id="yamlPreview">
type: process
name: telegram_collector
version: 1.0.0
inputs:
  - bot_token: string (req)
  - channels: list (req)
outputs:
  - messages: string
  - count: integer
schedule: "0 */6 * * *"
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">Statistics</div>
          <div class="stats-grid">
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Created</div>
              <div class="stat-value">2026-05-12</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Used</div>
              <div class="stat-value">15x</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Updated</div>
              <div class="stat-value">2h ago</div>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-item">
              <div class="stat-label">Version</div>
              <div class="stat-value">1.0.0</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="preview-section">
          <div class="preview-title">Dependencies</div>
          <div class="dep-text">Requires: http_node, agent, file_node<br>Used by: project_alpha, monitor</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // Auto-save simulation (every 30 seconds)
    let lastSave = Date.now();
    const autoSaveSpan = document.getElementById('autoSaveIndicator');
    setInterval(() => {
      lastSave = Date.now();
      autoSaveSpan.textContent = 'Saved';
      autoSaveSpan.style.color = 'var(--k-status-success)';
      setTimeout(() => {
        autoSaveSpan.textContent = 'Auto-saved';
      }, 2000);
    }, 30000);

    // Tab switching
    document.querySelectorAll('.editor-tab').forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.editor-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        tab.classList.add('active');
        if (tab.dataset.mode === 'yaml') {
          alert('YAML Editor mode - Monaco editor would appear here');
        }
      });
    });

    // Type switching
    document.querySelectorAll('.type-item').forEach(item => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.type-item').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        item.classList.add('active');
        alert('Switched to ' + item.dataset.type + ' editor');
      });
    });

    // Component selection
    document.querySelectorAll('.component-item').forEach(item => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.component-item').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
        item.classList.add('active');
        alert('Load component: ' + item.textContent);
      });
    });

    // Add input/output handlers
    document.getElementById('addInputBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const container = document.getElementById('inputsList');
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'array-item';
      div.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name">
        <select class="form-select" style="width: 90px;"><option>string</option><option>integer</option><option>list</option><option>secret</option></select>
        <label style="font-size: 11px;"><input type="checkbox"> req</label>
        <button class="btn-icon" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
      `;
      container.appendChild(div);
    });

    document.getElementById('addOutputBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const container = document.getElementById('outputsList');
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'array-item';
      div.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="form-input" placeholder="Name">
        <select class="form-select" style="width: 90px;"><option>string</option><option>integer</option><option>list</option><option>object</option></select>
        <button class="btn-icon" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
      `;
      container.appendChild(div);
    });

    // Add tag
    document.getElementById('addTagBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const input = document.getElementById('tagInput');
      const tag = input.value.trim();
      if (tag) {
        const container = document.getElementById('tagsContainer');
        const span = document.createElement('span');
        span.className = 'tag';
        span.textContent = tag;
        container.appendChild(span);
        input.value = '';
      }
    });

    // Save button
    document.getElementById('saveBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Component saved to localStorage');
    });

    // New button
    document.getElementById('newBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      alert('Create new component');
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

5. ✅ Для Skill — оставить только YAML редактор или нужна форма?

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

# СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПРОМПТ: ЦЕНТР УПРАВЛЕНИЯ ПРОЕКТАМИ KYMATICS (ЭТАП 85 — COMPONENTS LIBRARY)

## 🎯 КОНТЕКСТ

Успешно завершены этапы 49-84.

### ДОСТИГНУТЫЕ РЕЗУЛЬТАТЫ (этап 84):
✅ Библиотека шаблонов (таблица templates, CRUD API)
✅ 10 атомарных скиллов (http_get, bash_exec, file_read и др.)
✅ API /skills/list
✅ Механизм композиции TemplateResolver
✅ Автоматическое добавление поля task для агентов
✅ Вкладка Templates в UI (стилизована под Kymatics)

### ОБНАРУЖЕННЫЕ ПРОБЛЕМЫ И ОГРАНИЧЕНИЯ:
❌ Нет единой библиотеки для всех типов компонентов (Process, Agent, Skill, Template, Integration)
❌ Нет визуального редактора с формой и YAML режимом
❌ Нет системы черновиков (drafts) с автосохранением
❌ Нет 3-колоночного макета как в Process Designer
❌ Локальная модель 7B-14B на M4 24GB не справляется со сложными процессами

⚠️ Железо: M4 24GB → локальные LLM 7B-14B работают на пределе

💰 БЮДЖЕТ ЭТАПА 85: 2000 ₽ (контроль каждого запроса к API)

## 📋 ПРИОРИТЕТЫ ЭТАПА 85

### P0 (НАИВЫСШИЙ) — БАЗОВАЯ СТРУКТУРА COMPONENTS
@🏛️ Хранитель архитектуры + @🚀 Хранитель стабильности:

1. **Создать страницу Components с 3-колоночным макетом:**
   - Левая колонка: поиск, теги, типы, список компонентов, черновики
   - Центральная колонка: редактор (форма + YAML режим)
   - Правая колонка: превью YAML, статистика, зависимости

2. **Поддержка типов компонентов:**
   - Process (steps workflow, inputs, outputs, schedule)
   - Agent (model, temperature, system_prompt, task_template)
   - Skill (executor, config_template) — только YAML редактор
   - Template (полный YAML) — только YAML редактор
   - Integration (auth_type, endpoints, base_url, rate_limit)

3. **API для Components:**
   ```
   GET /api/v1/components/list?type=process
   GET /api/v1/components/{id}
   POST /api/v1/components/create
   PUT /api/v1/components/{id}
   DELETE /api/v1/components/{id}
   ```

### P1 (ВЫСОКИЙ) — СИСТЕМА ЧЕРНОВИКОВ
@🧠 Специалист по знаниям:

1. **Хранение черновиков:**
   - localStorage для быстрого доступа (клиентская часть)
   - SQLite таблица drafts (серверная часть) с полями:
     ```sql
     CREATE TABLE drafts (
       id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
       component_type TEXT NOT NULL,
       component_id TEXT,
       name TEXT,
       data TEXT NOT NULL,
       last_auto_save TIMESTAMP,
       created_at TIMESTAMP,
       updated_at TIMESTAMP
     )
     ```

2. **Механизм автосохранения:**
   - Каждые 30 секунд → сохранять в localStorage + API синхронизация
   - Индикатор статуса сохранения
   - Восстановление черновика при открытии

3. **Поиск по черновикам LLM:**
   - API: `POST /api/v1/drafts/search` — поиск похожих черновиков
   - LLM анализирует черновики и предлагает как кандидаты для доработки

### P2 (СРЕДНИЙ) — YAML РЕДАКТОР И ПРЕВЬЮ
@🏛️ Хранитель архитектуры:

1. **Интеграция Monaco Editor:**
   - Подсветка синтаксиса YAML
   - Валидация схемы
   - Переключение Form ↔ YAML с синхронизацией

2. **Живое превью:**
   - Обновление при изменении
   - Валидация структуры
   - Подсветка ошибок

### P3 (НИЗКИЙ) — ИНТЕГРАЦИЯ С API
@🚀 Хранитель стабильности:

1. **CRUD операции:**
   - Загрузка списка компонентов
   - Загрузка компонента по ID
   - Сохранение (создание/обновление)
   - Удаление

2. **Синхронизация черновиков:**
   - При старте загружать drafts из API
   - При сохранении отправлять в API
